# 30 · UI: public pages and the Thai-first admin

Planning document, October 2026. This file specifies every screen the backend adds: the public pages (`/book`,
`/privacy`, `/enrol/$token`, `/my/$token`), the small homepage changes, and the admin area under `/admin`. It also
holds **all new copy in Thai and English**, ready to paste. Server functions, schemas and error codes are in
`20-server-api.md`; names come from `00-overview.md` § 10.

> **Verified, not guessed.** Blocks marked ✅ were compiled on 2026-10-14 in a scratch copy of this repo together with
> the code in `20-server-api.md`: `npx vite build` ✓, `npx tsc --noEmit` exit 0, `npx eslint` 0 errors (only the
> existing `react-refresh/only-export-components` warnings), `npx vitest run` 57 passed, leak grep empty.
> Blocks marked 📝 are specifications to build with the same patterns.
>
> **October 2026 review (`00-overview.md` § 15).** Blocks edited after the compile run are marked **✅ (rev)**; the first
> card that builds one proves it with the README acceptance commands. Copy added by the review is in **§ 5.1**.
> Feature ids (`F1-05`) come from `00-overview.md` § 8; card ids (`P1-08`) from `90-task-breakdown.md`.
>
> **Copy status.** All parent-facing text is a draft for the studio owner to approve. The privacy notice and every
> consent wording also need a Thai PDPA lawyer (gates G9a, G9b). Don't add facts the studio hasn't published:
> no group sizes, lunch arrangements, refund/cancellation/expiry rules, outcomes or guarantees.

**Contents**
1. Ground rules for every screen
2. Shared building blocks
3. Public site
4. Admin area
5. Admin copy file (`src/content/admin.tsx`)
6. Accessibility, mobile and tests checklist
7. Names introduced by this file

---

## 1. Ground rules for every screen

### 1.1 Where copy lives

The task brief said "add keys to `src/content/site.tsx`". The architect's decision **D23** refines this, and this file
follows D23:

| Copy | File | Export |
|---|---|---|
| Homepage additions (F1-07) and the small shared subpage strings | `src/content/site.tsx` (diff in § 3.1) | `content` (existing) |
| `/book` | `src/content/booking.tsx` | `bookingContent: Record<Lang, BookingContent>` |
| `/privacy` | `src/content/privacy.tsx` | `privacyContent`, `PRIVACY_NOTICE_VERSION`, `CONTROLLER_LEGAL_NAME` |
| `/enrol/$token` | `src/content/enrol.tsx` | `enrolContent` |
| `/my/$token` | `src/content/my-consents.tsx` | `myConsentsContent` |
| Admin | `src/content/admin/index.ts` assembling one module per area: `core.tsx`, `inbox.tsx`, `settings.tsx`, `sessions.tsx`, `families.tsx`, `payments.tsx`, `health.tsx`, `consents.tsx`, `media.tsx`, `privacy.tsx`, `reports.tsx` (§ 5, R-17) | `adminContent` (used through `useAdminLanguage()`; import path `@/content/admin` is unchanged) |
| Consent wording (health, each media channel) | **database** `consent_texts` (seeded by `sql/0006_seed_consent_texts.sql`) | read by `getEnrolmentForm` / `getMyConsents` |

House rules (from `AGENTS.md`, apply to every string):
- Thai and English always change together. Thai studio voice ends with ค่ะ; warm, plain, non-judgmental, no
  fear-based wording.
- Wrap Thai compound words and names in `<Nobr>` (or `⁠` inside plain strings); mark English inside Thai with
  `<En>`. `En` is currently private in `site.tsx`; § 3.1 exports it.
- Say บอร์ดเกมการเงิน / board games, never "money game".
- No invented policies. Unknown → a field staff fill in, or "ask us on LINE".
- Page language: public pages use the existing `LanguageProvider` with `initialLang` from `?lang=`; links to other
  public pages add `?lang=en` when English is active.

### 1.2 Components to use

The public site uses its own paper-style classes (`.btn`, `.btn-primary`, `.btn-ghost`, `.btn-sm`, `.btn-lg`,
`.eyebrow`, `.section-lead`, `container-site`, `rounded-paper`, `shadow-cut-*`) and brand tokens from
`src/styles.css`. Use shadcn/ui (`src/components/ui/*`) for form controls and admin widgets:

| Need | Component | Size/contrast fix (always apply) |
|---|---|---|
| Text input | `Input` | `className="min-h-11 bg-paper text-base"` (default `h-9` is 36 px; `text-base` stops iOS zoom) |
| Long text | `Textarea` | `bg-paper text-base`, `maxLength` matching the schema |
| Single choice | `RadioGroup` + `RadioGroupItem` | wrap each in `<label className="flex min-h-11 items-center gap-3">`, item `h-5 w-5` |
| Yes/no, consent | `Checkbox` | wrap in a `<label>` row `min-h-11`; checkbox `h-6 w-6`; **never `defaultChecked`** |
| On/off setting (admin) | `Switch` | label row `min-h-11` |
| Age, month, year | native `<select>` | `min-h-11 w-full rounded-md border border-input bg-paper px-3 text-base` |
| Forms | `Form`, `FormField`, `FormItem`, `FormLabel`, `FormControl`, `FormDescription` + react-hook-form + `zodResolver` | Use `FieldError` (§ 2.2) instead of `FormMessage` |
| Tables (admin) | `Table*` | hide secondary columns below `sm`/`md`; first column is a link with `min-h-11` |
| Status | `Badge` | `variant="secondary"`; failures `variant="destructive"` |
| Dialogs | `Dialog` (forms), `AlertDialog` (destructive confirmations), `Sheet` (edit panels on phones) | |
| Filters | `Tabs` | `TabsTrigger className="min-h-11"`, wrap with `flex-wrap` |
| Menus | `DropdownMenu` | items `min-h-11` |
| Toasts (admin only) | `Toaster` from `@/components/ui/sonner` + `toast` from `sonner` | never put personal data in a toast |
| Loading | `Skeleton` | |

Error text colour: `text-cherry-deep` (≥ 4.5:1 on cream and paper). Don't use `text-destructive` for small text, and
never use the decorative colours (`dusty`, `mustard`, `sage`, `coral`, `cherry-logo`) for text.

### 1.3 Form pattern (public and admin)

1. Labels are real `<label>`/`FormLabel` elements, never placeholders. Hints use `FormDescription` (linked by
   `aria-describedby` automatically).
2. Validate with the **same zod schema** the server uses (`zodResolver`). Zod messages are codes; `FieldError` maps
   them to copy.
3. On submit with errors: focus moves to the first invalid field (`shouldFocusError: true`), and an error summary
   with `role="alert"` says how many fields need a fix.
4. Server-side failures (`PublicResult.code`) show a message above the submit button (`role="alert"`) or the LINE
   fallback panel. Never lose what the parent typed on a recoverable error.
5. Buttons show a busy label while submitting and are disabled to prevent double submits.
6. Nothing personal goes into a URL (no names, phones, LINE IDs in query strings or LINE deep links; the ref code is fine).

### 2.2 `FieldError` ✅ (`src/components/forms/FieldError.tsx`)

```tsx
import { useFormField } from "@/components/ui/form";

/**
 * Replaces shadcn <FormMessage> on public and admin forms: zod messages are CODES
 * ("invalid_phone"), so look them up in the page's copy. Keeps the id that <FormControl>
 * points aria-describedby at. Uses text-cherry-deep (≥ 4.5:1 on cream and paper).
 */
export function FieldError({ messages }: { messages: Record<string, string> }) {
  const { error, formMessageId } = useFormField();
  if (!error) return null;
  const code = String(error.message ?? "");
  return (
    <p id={formMessageId} className="mt-1 text-sm font-medium text-cherry-deep">
      {messages[code] ?? messages["invalid"] ?? code}
    </p>
  );
}
```

### 2.3 `SubpageHeader` ✅ (`src/components/SubpageHeader.tsx`)

```tsx
import { LineIcon } from "./icons";
import { ExternalLink } from "./ExternalLink";
import { LINE_URL } from "@/content/business";
import { useLanguage } from "@/i18n/LanguageProvider";
import type { Lang } from "@/content/site";

const LANG_OPTIONS: { code: Lang; label: string }[] = [
  { code: "th", label: "ไทย" },
  { code: "en", label: "EN" },
];

/**
 * Header for /book, /privacy, /enrol/$token, /my/$token. Logo → home (keeps the language),
 * TH/EN switch, and LINE as the primary action. No section nav (those anchors live on the home page).
 */
export function SubpageHeader({ showLine = true }: { showLine?: boolean }) {
  const { lang, setLang, t } = useLanguage();
  const home = lang === "en" ? "/?lang=en" : "/";
  return (
    <header className="site-header sticky top-0 z-50 border-b-2 border-peach-deep bg-cream">
      <div className="container-site flex min-h-[72px] items-center gap-3">
        <a
          href={home}
          aria-label={t.subpage.backHome}
          className="mr-auto inline-flex min-h-11 items-center gap-2 no-underline"
        >
          <img
            src="/images/logo-mark.png"
            width={215}
            height={226}
            alt=""
            className="h-10 w-auto"
          />
          <img
            src="/images/logo-wordmark.png"
            width={383}
            height={53}
            alt=""
            className="hidden h-[16px] w-auto xs:block"
          />
        </a>
        <div
          role="group"
          aria-label={t.nav.langGroup}
          className="inline-flex rounded-full border-2 border-ink bg-paper p-0.5"
        >
          {LANG_OPTIONS.map((opt) => (
            <button
              key={opt.code}
              type="button"
              lang={opt.code}
              aria-pressed={lang === opt.code}
              onClick={() => setLang(opt.code)}
              className={`min-h-11 min-w-11 cursor-pointer rounded-full px-2.5 font-display text-[0.9rem] font-semibold ${
                lang === opt.code ? "bg-ink text-cream" : "hover:bg-cherry-soft"
              }`}
            >
              {opt.label}
            </button>
          ))}
        </div>
        {showLine && (
          <ExternalLink
            href={LINE_URL}
            className="btn btn-primary btn-sm hidden whitespace-nowrap sm:inline-flex"
          >
            <LineIcon />
            {t.nav.cta}
          </ExternalLink>
        )}
      </div>
    </header>
  );
}
```

Subpages render `<SubpageHeader />`, `<main id="main" className="container-site max-w-[760px] py-10 md:py-16">`,
then `<Footer hrefPrefix="/" />`. The home page keeps its own `Header`, `StickyCta` and `Footer` unchanged.

### 2.4 Prose styles for the privacy page (F1-02) 📝

Tailwind's reset removes list bullets and paragraph spacing. Add to `src/styles.css` inside the existing
`@layer components { … }`:

```css
  /* Long-form text (privacy notice) */
  .privacy-prose p {
    @apply mb-3;
  }
  .privacy-prose ul {
    @apply mb-3 grid list-disc gap-1 pl-5;
  }
  /* "Who we share data with" and "How long we keep data" are tables (R-28) */
  .privacy-prose table {
    @apply mb-3 w-full border-collapse text-sm;
  }
  .privacy-prose th,
  .privacy-prose td {
    @apply border-b border-ink-line px-2 py-2 text-left align-top;
  }
```

In `privacy.tsx`, wrap each `<table>` in `<div className="overflow-x-auto">` so a 360 px screen never scrolls the page
sideways, and give each table a `<caption className="sr-only">` with the section title.

### 2.5 `TurnstileWidget` ✅ (`src/components/booking/TurnstileWidget.tsx`)

**Allowed hostname (rev 14 Oct 2026, decision 9):** `cherryleeandco.com` only. The Cloudflare widget lists nothing else (`20-server-api.md` § 7.4).


Loads Cloudflare's script only on `/book`, renders explicitly, removes itself on unmount, and gives a fresh token
after a failed submit (tokens are single-use). Uses `turnstileSiteKey()` from `public-config.ts` (test key in dev).

```tsx
import { useEffect, useRef } from "react";
import { turnstileSiteKey } from "@/lib/backend/public-config";

type TurnstileApi = {
  render: (
    el: HTMLElement,
    opts: {
      sitekey: string;
      action: string;
      language: string;
      theme: "light";
      callback: (token: string) => void;
      "expired-callback": () => void;
      "error-callback": () => void;
    },
  ) => string;
  remove: (id: string) => void;
  reset: (id: string) => void;
};
declare global {
  interface Window {
    turnstile?: TurnstileApi;
  }
}

const SCRIPT_SRC = "https://challenges.cloudflare.com/turnstile/v0/api.js?render=explicit";
let scriptPromise: Promise<void> | null = null;

function loadScript(): Promise<void> {
  if (window.turnstile) return Promise.resolve();
  scriptPromise ??= new Promise<void>((resolve, reject) => {
    const s = document.createElement("script");
    s.src = SCRIPT_SRC;
    s.async = true;
    s.defer = true;
    s.onload = () => resolve();
    s.onerror = () => {
      scriptPromise = null;
      reject(new Error("turnstile_script_failed"));
    };
    document.head.appendChild(s);
  });
  return scriptPromise;
}

type Props = {
  action: "booking_request";
  lang: "th" | "en";
  /** token, or null when expired / failed */
  onToken: (token: string | null) => void;
  /** Script blocked or failed: the form shows its LINE fallback */
  onUnavailable: () => void;
  /** Increment to get a fresh token after a failed submit (tokens are single-use) */
  resetSignal: number;
};

export function TurnstileWidget({ action, lang, onToken, onUnavailable, resetSignal }: Props) {
  const box = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);
  const widgetId = useRef<string | null>(null);
  const cb = useRef({ onToken, onUnavailable });
  cb.current = { onToken, onUnavailable };

  useEffect(() => {
    let cancelled = false;
    loadScript()
      .then(() => {
        if (cancelled || !box.current || !window.turnstile) return;
        widgetId.current = window.turnstile.render(box.current, {
          sitekey: turnstileSiteKey(),
          action,
          language: lang,
          theme: "light",
          callback: (t) => cb.current.onToken(t),
          "expired-callback": () => cb.current.onToken(null),
          "error-callback": () => cb.current.onToken(null),
        });
      })
      .catch(() => cb.current.onUnavailable());
    return () => {
      cancelled = true;
      if (widgetId.current && window.turnstile) window.turnstile.remove(widgetId.current);
      widgetId.current = null;
    };
  }, [action, lang]);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (resetSignal > 0 && widgetId.current && window.turnstile) {
      window.turnstile.reset(widgetId.current);
      cb.current.onToken(null);
    }
  }, [resetSignal]);

  return <div ref={box} className="min-h-[65px]" />;
}
```

### 2.6 `MarketingConsentBlock` (rev 14 Oct 2026; cards P3-11, P3-14, P3-20, P4-22; owner decision 2)

The one component for the marketing question. It appears in three places: the convert dialog (`/admin/inbox/$requestId`, P3-14), the guardian sheet (`/admin/families/$familyId`, P3-11) and `/my` (P4-22). File `src/components/consent/MarketingConsentBlock.tsx`.

Props: `{ text: MarketingLineText | null; mode: "ask" | "manage"; granted: boolean; decidedAt: string | null; checked: boolean;
onChecked(next: boolean): void; onWithdraw?(): void; lang: Lang }`. `MarketingLineText` is from `families.schema.ts`.

- **Text first.** The full `text.bodyMd` renders with `ConsentTextBody` **directly above** the control, never behind a link or a
  collapsible, under the heading `marketingLine.textHeading`.
- **Unticked by default.** `checked` starts `false` and is never set from `granted`.
- `mode = "ask"` (no earlier choice): one unticked `Checkbox` labelled `marketingLine.label`, and `marketingLine.notNow` under it.
- `mode = "manage"` (a choice exists): the status line (`marketingLine.statusGranted(date)` or `marketingLine.statusNotGranted`).
  When not granted, show the unticked checkbox. When granted, show **no** checkbox, only the button `marketingLine.withdraw`
  (one press, no confirm, ≥ 44 px, `onWithdraw`). Withdrawal is as easy as giving (PDPA s.19).
- `text = null` (not published yet): show only `marketingLine.noText`. No checkbox, and the caller sends no marketing data.
- Always: `marketingLine.childNote` under the box. A channel radio (`marketingLine.channel.*`: in person / LINE) appears
  next to the checkbox on staff screens, with no default: staff pick one. `/my` has no radio; it always records `web`.
- Nothing from this box goes into a URL, a log or a query string.

Tests: the text is in the DOM and visible above the checkbox; the checkbox starts unchecked, also when `granted` is true; in
`manage` mode with `granted`, no checkbox exists and the withdraw button calls `onWithdraw` once.

## 3. Public site

LINE stays the **primary** call to action everywhere. The form is a secondary, quieter option for parents who
can't chat right now.

### 3.1 Homepage entry points (F1-07) 📝

Copy keys are in the diff in § 2.1 (`cta.formLink`, `pricing.formLink`, and the FAQ "จองที่นั่งอย่างไร? / How do I
book?" answer). Component changes:

- `src/components/sections/CtaBand.tsx`: under the two buttons, add a text link (not a button) on its own line:
  `<a href={lang === "en" ? "/book?lang=en" : "/book"} className="mt-6 inline-flex min-h-11 items-center text-white underline hover:text-white">{c.formLink}</a>`
  (white on cherry passes 5.5:1; the band already sets white focus outlines).
- `src/components/sections/Pricing.tsx`: below the plan cards grid and above "ทุกแพ็กเกจรวมแล้ว", a centred text link
  `{p.formLink}` to `/book` (+ `?lang=en`). Keep the LINE buttons on each card exactly as they are.
- `src/components/sections/Faq.tsx`: no change (the answer is a ReactNode in `site.tsx`).
- Optional (F2-04): an "open dates" strip under the pricing title, see § 3.3.
- Optional (F6-03, only when Umami is enabled at G12): `data-umami-event="book-form-open"` on these two form links
  and `data-umami-event="book-line-click"` on the existing LINE buttons. No other attributes, no personal data.

### 3.2 `/book` (F1-05) ✅

Files: `src/routes/book.tsx`, `src/content/booking.tsx`, `src/components/booking/BookingForm.tsx`,
`BookingThanks.tsx`, `TurnstileWidget.tsx`, plus `SubpageHeader`, `Footer`, `FieldError`.
Search params: `?lang=th|en`, `?package=single|four|eight` (preselects the package), `?session=<uuid>` (P2).
The page renders on the server without touching the database (D15); Turnstile loads in the browser.

Layout (one column, max 760 px):

1. Eyebrow "Witty English Weekends", `h1`, lead ("this isn't a booking or a payment yet").
2. **LINE-first box** (sage background): "Want to talk now? Message us on LINE" + primary LINE button.
3. The form, in four fieldsets: contact → children → package and day → message.
4. Turnstile widget, short privacy notice with a link to `/privacy`, submit button. **No consent checkbox** (D8).

| # | Field | Control | Rules (zod code → message key) |
|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | `guardianName` "ชื่อที่ให้เราเรียกคุณ / What should we call you?" | `Input autoComplete="name"` | required, ≤ 80 (`required`, `too_long`) |
| 2 | `contactPhone` | `Input type="tel" inputMode="tel"` | Thai phone, normalised (`invalid_phone`); **required only when `preferredContact = phone`** (`preferred_missing`, shown under this field) |
| 3 | `contactLineId` (optional matching hint, D26) | `Input autoCapitalize="none"` | `LINE_ID_PATTERN` (`invalid_line_id`) |
| — | how LINE works | `contactNote` above the fields: LINE = you send us your code after this form; we can't message a LINE ID first | — |
| 4 | `preferredContact` LINE / phone | `RadioGroup`, default LINE | no rule beyond field 2 (D26) |
| 5 | `children[]` 1–3: nickname (optional, ≤ 30), age 6–12 | repeater; native `<select>` for age with no default | `age_required`, `too_many_children` |
| 6 | `packageCode` 1 / 4 / 8 sessions / not sure | `RadioGroup`, prefilled from `?package=`, default "not sure" | — |
| 7 | `preferredDay` Sat / Sun / either | `RadioGroup`, no default | — |
| 8 | (P2) `sessionId` | `SessionPicker` (§ 3.3) | — |
| 9 | `message` (optional, ≤ 500) with the hint **"กรุณาอย่าใส่ข้อมูลสุขภาพในช่องนี้ เราจะสอบถามแยกต่างหากค่ะ"** | `Textarea` + live counter | `too_long` |
| — | honeypot `company_website` | off-screen, `aria-hidden`, `tabIndex={-1}` | never shown |

States:

| State | What the parent sees |
|---|---|
| Idle | Form; submit disabled until Turnstile gives a token, with "รอระบบยืนยันสักครู่นะคะ…" under the button |
| Field errors | Summary `role="alert"` ("ยังมี 2 ช่องที่ต้องแก้ไขค่ะ") + messages under fields; focus on the first |
| Submitting | Button "กำลังส่ง…", disabled |
| `ok` | `BookingThanks`: focus moves to the heading; ref code (large, selectable, `lang="en"`); **"ส่งรหัสทาง LINE"** button (the main action: parents who chose LINE must send it, D26) = `lineMessageUrl("สวัสดีครับ/ค่ะ ขอส่งรหัสคำขอจอง CL-XXXXX")`; copy button; "What happens next" list (no private-link promise in Stage A, R-24) |
| `too_fast`, `invalid_input` | Message above the button; form keeps its values |
| `bot_check_failed`, `rate_limited`, `form_closed`, `server_error`, network error | **LINE fallback panel** (`role="alert"`): the specific message, then "Message us on LINE instead" with a LINE link whose prefilled text has **no personal data**; "Try sending again" except for `form_closed`/`rate_limited` |
| Turnstile script blocked | Same fallback panel (`bot_check_failed`) |

✅ (rev) `src/content/booking.tsx` (D26 contact copy, R-24 no unconfirmed promises):

```tsx
import type { ReactNode } from "react";
import { PRICES, formatBaht } from "./business";
import { En, Nobr, type Lang } from "./site";
import type { PublicErrorCode } from "@/lib/backend/result";

/**
 * Copy for /book (F1-05, F2-04). Thai + English side by side; components only handle layout.
 * The form is a request, not a booking or a consent (D8): no checkbox, a short notice + /privacy link.
 */

/** Zod message codes used by BookingFormSchema (src/lib/backend/booking.schema.ts) */
export type BookingFieldErrorCode =
  | "required"
  | "too_long"
  | "invalid_phone"
  | "invalid_line_id"
  | "preferred_missing"
  | "age_required"
  | "child_required"
  | "too_many_children"
  | "invalid";

export type BookingContent = {
  meta: { title: string; description: string };
  intro: {
    eyebrow: string;
    title: ReactNode;
    lead: ReactNode;
    /** Shown above the form: LINE is still the fastest way */
    lineFirst: ReactNode;
    lineButton: string;
  };
  sections: { you: string; children: string; interest: string; message: string };
  fields: {
    guardianName: { label: string; hint: string };
    contactPhone: { label: string; hint: string };
    contactLineId: { label: string; hint: string };
    contactNote: string;
    preferredContact: { legend: string; line: string; phone: string };
    child: {
      heading: (n: number) => string;
      nickname: { label: string; hint: string };
      age: { label: string; placeholder: string; option: (age: number) => string };
      add: string;
      remove: (n: number) => string;
      max: string;
    };
    packageCode: {
      legend: string;
      single: string;
      four: string;
      eight: string;
      notSure: string;
    };
    preferredDay: { legend: string; sat: string; sun: string; either: string };
    message: { label: string; hint: string; counter: (n: number) => string };
  };
  /** F2-04: open dates fetched in the browser after render (D15) */
  sessions: {
    legend: string;
    hint: string;
    loading: string;
    unavailable: ReactNode;
    none: ReactNode;
    noPreference: string;
    seatsLeft: (n: number) => string;
    open: string;
    full: string;
    waitlistNote: string;
  };
  /** OWNER-APPROVE with the …-v1 notice (G9a · v1, G0 Q6): states the PROPOSED 6-month retention (00 § 12) */
  notice: ReactNode;
  botCheck: { label: string; waiting: string };
  /** Label of the off-screen honeypot input (never seen by people; every input still has a label) */
  honeypotLabel: string;
  submit: string;
  submitting: string;
  errorSummary: (n: number) => string;
  fieldErrors: Record<BookingFieldErrorCode, string>;
  formErrors: Record<
    Exclude<PublicErrorCode, "link_invalid" | "link_expired" | "link_used">,
    string
  >;
  fallback: {
    title: string;
    body: ReactNode;
    lineButton: string;
    lineMessage: string;
    retry: string;
  };
  thanks: {
    title: string;
    body: ReactNode;
    refLabel: string;
    copy: string;
    copied: string;
    lineButton: string;
    /** No personal data in a LINE deep link — the ref code only */
    lineMessage: (refCode: string) => string;
    nextTitle: string;
    next: ReactNode[];
    home: string;
  };
};

      "We couldn’t complete the robot check. Please try again, or message us on LINE.",
    rate_limited:
      "We’ve had several requests from this network already. Please message us on LINE instead.",
    form_closed: "The form is paused for now. Please message us on LINE.",
    server_error:
      "Sorry, something went wrong on our side. Please try again, or message us on LINE.",
  },
  fallback: {
    title: "Message us on LINE instead",
    body: "Your details haven’t reached us yet. Tap the button below to message us on LINE and tell us what you’re looking for.",
    lineButton: "Message us on LINE",
    lineMessage: "Hi! I’m interested in Witty English Weekends. Could you share available dates?",
    retry: "Try sending again",
  },
  thanks: {
    title: "Thank you, we’ve got your details",
    body: "If you chose LINE, tap “Send the code on LINE” below to start a chat with us: the studio’s LINE account can only reply after you message us. If you asked for a call back, we’ll call the number you gave.",
    refLabel: "Your request code",
    copy: "Copy code",
    copied: "Copied",
    lineButton: "Send the code on LINE",
    lineMessage: (ref) => `Hi! Here’s my booking request code: ${ref}`,
    nextTitle: "What happens next",
    next: [
      "We talk with you to help you choose a date and package.",
      "We confirm the seat and payment with you in the chat or by phone.",
    ],
    home: "Back to home",
  },
};

export const bookingContent: Record<Lang, BookingContent> = { th, en };
```

✅ (rev) `src/components/booking/BookingForm.tsx` (rev, R-45: each `RadioGroup` gets `aria-labelledby` pointing at its
`FormLabel` id, because `FormLabel`'s `htmlFor` targets the group's `div`, which is not labelable; the honeypot label
comes from `bookingContent` instead of a hard-coded string):

```tsx
import { zodResolver } from "@hookform/resolvers/zod";
import { useServerFn } from "@tanstack/react-start";
import { useRef, useState } from "react";
import { useFieldArray, useForm } from "react-hook-form";
import { FieldError } from "@/components/forms/FieldError";
import {
  Form,
  FormControl,
  FormDescription,
  FormField,
  FormItem,
  FormLabel,
} from "@/components/ui/form";
import { Input } from "@/components/ui/input";
import { RadioGroup, RadioGroupItem } from "@/components/ui/radio-group";
import { Textarea } from "@/components/ui/textarea";
import { bookingContent } from "@/content/booking";
import { PRIVACY_NOTICE_VERSION } from "@/content/privacy";
import { useLanguage } from "@/i18n/LanguageProvider";
import { submitBookingRequest } from "@/lib/backend/booking.functions";
import { BookingFormSchema, type BookingFormValues } from "@/lib/backend/booking.schema";
import type { PublicErrorCode } from "@/lib/backend/result";
import { TurnstileWidget } from "./TurnstileWidget";

type Props = {
  initialPackage: BookingFormValues["packageCode"];
  onDone: (refCode: string) => void;
  /** Codes that need the LINE fallback panel (bot_check_failed, rate_limited, form_closed, server_error) */
  onFallback: (code: PublicErrorCode) => void;
};

const AGES = [6, 7, 8, 9, 10, 11, 12] as const;
const PACKAGES = ["single", "four", "eight"] as const;
const INPUT = "min-h-11 bg-paper text-base";

export function BookingForm({ initialPackage, onDone, onFallback }: Props) {
  const { lang } = useLanguage();
  const c = bookingContent[lang];
  const submit = useServerFn(submitBookingRequest);
  const renderedAt = useRef(Date.now());
  const honeypot = useRef<HTMLInputElement>(null);
  const [token, setToken] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [resetSignal, setResetSignal] = useState(0);
  const [formError, setFormError] = useState<string | null>(null);

  const form = useForm<BookingFormValues>({
    resolver: zodResolver(BookingFormSchema),
    defaultValues: {
      guardianName: "",
      contactPhone: "",
      contactLineId: "",
      preferredContact: "line",
      children: [{ nickname: "", ageYears: Number.NaN }],
      packageCode: initialPackage,
      preferredDay: null,
      preferredDate: null,
      sessionId: null,
      message: "",
    },
    shouldFocusError: true,
  });
  const kids = useFieldArray({ control: form.control, name: "children" });
  const errorCount = Object.keys(form.formState.errors).length;

  async function onSubmit(values: BookingFormValues) {
    setFormError(null);
    let result;
    try {
      result = await submit({
        data: {
          ...values,
          lang,
          privacyNoticeVersion: PRIVACY_NOTICE_VERSION,
          companyWebsite: honeypot.current?.value ?? "",
          fillMs: Date.now() - renderedAt.current,
          turnstileToken: token ?? "",
        },
      });
    } catch {
      result = { ok: false as const, code: "server_error" as const }; // network down, Cloud paused…
    }
    if (result.ok) return onDone(result.data.refCode);
    setResetSignal((n) => n + 1); // Turnstile tokens are single-use
    if (result.code === "too_fast" || result.code === "invalid_input") {
      setFormError(c.formErrors[result.code]);
    } else if (
      result.code === "bot_check_failed" ||
      result.code === "rate_limited" ||
      result.code === "form_closed" ||
      result.code === "server_error"
    ) {
      onFallback(result.code);
    }
  }

  return (
    <Form {...form}>
      <form onSubmit={form.handleSubmit(onSubmit)} noValidate className="grid gap-8">
        {/* Error summary: announced once per submit; field messages sit under each field */}
        <div role="alert" aria-live="assertive" className="empty:hidden">
          {form.formState.submitCount > 0 && errorCount > 0 ? (
            <p className="rounded-xl border-2 border-cherry-deep bg-cherry-soft px-4 py-3 font-medium text-ink">
              {c.errorSummary(errorCount)}
            </p>
          ) : null}
          {formError ? (
            <p className="rounded-xl border-2 border-cherry-deep bg-cherry-soft px-4 py-3 font-medium text-ink">
              {formError}
            </p>
          ) : null}
        </div>

        <fieldset className="grid gap-5">
          <legend className="mb-2 font-display text-h3">{c.sections.you}</legend>
          <FormField
            control={form.control}
            name="guardianName"
            render={({ field }) => (
              <FormItem>
                <FormLabel>{c.fields.guardianName.label}</FormLabel>
                <FormControl>
                  <Input {...field} autoComplete="name" maxLength={80} className={INPUT} />
                </FormControl>
                <FormDescription>{c.fields.guardianName.hint}</FormDescription>
                <FieldError messages={c.fieldErrors} />
              </FormItem>
            )}
          />
          <p className="text-sm text-ink-soft">{c.fields.contactNote}</p>
          <div className="grid gap-5 sm:grid-cols-2">
            <FormField
              control={form.control}
              name="contactPhone"
              render={({ field }) => (
                <FormItem>
                  <FormLabel>{c.fields.contactPhone.label}</FormLabel>
                  <FormControl>
                    <Input
                      {...field}
                      value={field.value ?? ""}
                      type="tel"
                      inputMode="tel"
                      autoComplete="tel"
                      className={INPUT}
                    />
                  </FormControl>
                  <FormDescription>{c.fields.contactPhone.hint}</FormDescription>
                  <FieldError messages={c.fieldErrors} />
                </FormItem>
              )}
            />
            <FormField
              control={form.control}
              name="contactLineId"
              render={({ field }) => (
                <FormItem>
                  <FormLabel>{c.fields.contactLineId.label}</FormLabel>
                  <FormControl>
                    <Input
                      {...field}
                      value={field.value ?? ""}
                      autoCapitalize="none"
                      autoCorrect="off"
                      spellCheck={false}
                      className={INPUT}
                    />
                  </FormControl>
                  <FormDescription>{c.fields.contactLineId.hint}</FormDescription>
                  <FieldError messages={c.fieldErrors} />
                </FormItem>
              )}
            />
          </div>
          <FormField
            control={form.control}
            name="preferredContact"
            render={({ field }) => (
              <FormItem>
                <FormLabel id="preferred-contact-label">{c.fields.preferredContact.legend}</FormLabel>
                <FormControl>
                  <RadioGroup
                    aria-labelledby="preferred-contact-label"
                    value={field.value}
                    onValueChange={field.onChange}
                    className="flex flex-wrap gap-x-6"
                  >
                    {(["line", "phone"] as const).map((v) => (
                      <label key={v} className="flex min-h-11 cursor-pointer items-center gap-3">
                        <RadioGroupItem value={v} className="h-5 w-5" />
                        {c.fields.preferredContact[v]}
                      </label>
                    ))}
                  </RadioGroup>
                </FormControl>
                <FieldError messages={c.fieldErrors} />
              </FormItem>
            )}
          />
        </fieldset>

        <fieldset className="grid gap-5">
          <legend className="mb-2 font-display text-h3">{c.sections.children}</legend>
          {kids.fields.map((row, i) => (
            <div
              key={row.id}
              className="grid gap-4 rounded-2xl border-2 border-peach-deep bg-paper p-4 sm:grid-cols-[1fr_10rem]"
            >
              <p className="font-display font-semibold sm:col-span-2">
                {c.fields.child.heading(i + 1)}
              </p>
              <FormField
                control={form.control}
                name={`children.${i}.nickname`}
                render={({ field }) => (
                  <FormItem>
                    <FormLabel>{c.fields.child.nickname.label}</FormLabel>
                    <FormControl>
                      <Input
                        {...field}
                        value={field.value ?? ""}
                        maxLength={30}
                        autoComplete="off"
                        className={INPUT}
                      />
                    </FormControl>
                    <FormDescription>{c.fields.child.nickname.hint}</FormDescription>
                    <FieldError messages={c.fieldErrors} />
                  </FormItem>
                )}
              />
              <FormField
                control={form.control}
                name={`children.${i}.ageYears`}
                render={({ field }) => (
                  <FormItem>
                    <FormLabel>{c.fields.child.age.label}</FormLabel>
                    <FormControl>
                      {/* Native select: best on phones, fully accessible */}
                      <select
                        value={Number.isFinite(field.value) ? String(field.value) : ""}
                        onChange={(e) =>
                          field.onChange(e.target.value ? Number(e.target.value) : Number.NaN)
                        }
                        onBlur={field.onBlur}
                        ref={field.ref}
                        className="min-h-11 w-full rounded-md border border-input bg-paper px-3 text-base"
                      >
                        <option value="">{c.fields.child.age.placeholder}</option>
                        {AGES.map((a) => (
                          <option key={a} value={a}>
                            {c.fields.child.age.option(a)}
                          </option>
                        ))}
                      </select>
                    </FormControl>
                    <FieldError messages={c.fieldErrors} />
                  </FormItem>
                )}
              />
              {kids.fields.length > 1 && (
                <button
                  type="button"
                  className="btn btn-ghost btn-sm justify-self-start sm:col-span-2"
                  onClick={() => kids.remove(i)}
                >
                  {c.fields.child.remove(i + 1)}
                </button>
              )}
            </div>
          ))}
          {kids.fields.length < 3 ? (
            <button
              type="button"
              className="btn btn-ghost btn-sm justify-self-start"
              onClick={() => kids.append({ nickname: "", ageYears: Number.NaN })}
            >
              {c.fields.child.add}
            </button>
          ) : (
            <p className="text-sm text-ink-soft">{c.fields.child.max}</p>
          )}
        </fieldset>

        <fieldset className="grid gap-5">
          <legend className="mb-2 font-display text-h3">{c.sections.interest}</legend>
          <FormField
            control={form.control}
            name="packageCode"
            render={({ field }) => (
              <FormItem>
                <FormLabel id="package-code-label">{c.fields.packageCode.legend}</FormLabel>
                <FormControl>
                  <RadioGroup
                    aria-labelledby="package-code-label"
                    value={field.value ?? "not_sure"}
                    onValueChange={(v) => field.onChange(v === "not_sure" ? null : v)}
                    className="grid gap-1"
                  >
                    {[...PACKAGES, "not_sure" as const].map((v) => (
                      <label key={v} className="flex min-h-11 cursor-pointer items-center gap-3">
                        <RadioGroupItem value={v} className="h-5 w-5" />
                        {v === "not_sure" ? c.fields.packageCode.notSure : c.fields.packageCode[v]}
                      </label>
                    ))}
                  </RadioGroup>
                </FormControl>
              </FormItem>
            )}
          />
          <FormField
            control={form.control}
            name="preferredDay"
            render={({ field }) => (
              <FormItem>
                <FormLabel id="preferred-day-label">{c.fields.preferredDay.legend}</FormLabel>
                <FormControl>
                  <RadioGroup
                    aria-labelledby="preferred-day-label"
                    value={field.value ?? ""}
                    onValueChange={field.onChange}
                    className="flex flex-wrap gap-x-6"
                  >
                    {(["sat", "sun", "either"] as const).map((v) => (
                      <label key={v} className="flex min-h-11 cursor-pointer items-center gap-3">
                        <RadioGroupItem value={v} className="h-5 w-5" />
                        {c.fields.preferredDay[v]}
                      </label>
                    ))}
                  </RadioGroup>
                </FormControl>
              </FormItem>
            )}
          />
          {/* F2-04: <SessionPicker /> replaces the free date with open sessions (see 30-ui.md § 3.3) */}
        </fieldset>

        <FormField
          control={form.control}
          name="message"
          render={({ field }) => (
            <FormItem>
              <FormLabel className="font-display text-h3">{c.fields.message.label}</FormLabel>
              <FormDescription>{c.fields.message.hint}</FormDescription>
              <FormControl>
                <Textarea
                  {...field}
                  value={field.value ?? ""}
                  rows={4}
                  maxLength={500}
                  className="bg-paper text-base"
                />
              </FormControl>
              <p className="text-right text-sm text-ink-soft" aria-hidden="true">
                {c.fields.message.counter((field.value ?? "").length)}
              </p>
              <FieldError messages={c.fieldErrors} />
            </FormItem>
          )}
        />

        {/* Honeypot: off-screen, hidden from assistive tech, skipped by keyboard */}
        <div aria-hidden="true" className="absolute -left-[9999px] h-px w-px overflow-hidden">
          <label htmlFor="company_website">{c.honeypotLabel}</label>
          <input
            ref={honeypot}
            id="company_website"
            name="company_website"
            type="text"
            tabIndex={-1}
            autoComplete="off"
          />
        </div>

        <div className="grid gap-4">
          <TurnstileWidget
            action="booking_request"
            lang={lang}
            onToken={setToken}
            onUnavailable={() => onFallback("bot_check_failed")}
            resetSignal={resetSignal}
          />
          <p className="text-sm text-ink-soft">{c.notice}</p>
          <button
            type="submit"
            className="btn btn-primary btn-lg w-full sm:w-auto"
            disabled={form.formState.isSubmitting || !token}
            aria-describedby={!token ? "bot-check-waiting" : undefined}
          >
            {form.formState.isSubmitting ? c.submitting : c.submit}
          </button>
          {!token && (
            <p id="bot-check-waiting" className="text-sm text-ink-soft">
              {c.botCheck.waiting}
            </p>
          )}
        </div>
      </form>
    </Form>
  );
}
```

✅ `src/components/booking/BookingThanks.tsx`:

```tsx
import { useEffect, useRef, useState } from "react";
import { ExternalLink } from "@/components/ExternalLink";
import { LineIcon } from "@/components/icons";
import { lineMessageUrl } from "@/content/business";
import { bookingContent } from "@/content/booking";
import { useLanguage } from "@/i18n/LanguageProvider";

export function BookingThanks({ refCode }: { refCode: string }) {
  const { lang } = useLanguage();
  const c = bookingContent[lang].thanks;
  const heading = useRef<HTMLHeadingElement>(null);
  const [copied, setCopied] = useState(false);

  // Move focus to the result so screen-reader and keyboard users land on it
  useEffect(() => heading.current?.focus(), []);

  async function copy() {
    try {
      await navigator.clipboard.writeText(refCode);
      setCopied(true);
    } catch {
      /* clipboard blocked: the code stays visible and selectable */
    }
  }

  return (
    <section aria-labelledby="thanks-title" className="grid gap-6">
      <h1 id="thanks-title" ref={heading} tabIndex={-1} className="text-h2">
        {c.title}
      </h1>
      <p className="section-lead">{c.body}</p>
      <div className="rounded-paper border-2 border-ink bg-paper p-6 text-center shadow-cut-peach">
        <p className="mb-1 text-sm text-ink-soft">{c.refLabel}</p>
        <p
          className="mb-4 select-all font-display text-[2rem] font-bold tracking-[0.08em]"
          lang="en"
        >
          {refCode}
        </p>
        <div className="flex flex-wrap justify-center gap-3">
          <ExternalLink href={lineMessageUrl(c.lineMessage(refCode))} className="btn btn-primary">
            <LineIcon />
            {c.lineButton}
          </ExternalLink>
          <button type="button" onClick={() => void copy()} className="btn btn-ghost">
            {copied ? c.copied : c.copy}
          </button>
        </div>
        <p role="status" className="sr-only">
          {copied ? c.copied : ""}
        </p>
      </div>
      <div>
        <h2 className="mb-3 text-h3">{c.nextTitle}</h2>
        <ol className="grid list-decimal gap-2 pl-6">
          {c.next.map((step, i) => (
            <li key={i}>{step}</li>
          ))}
        </ol>
      </div>
      <a href={lang === "en" ? "/?lang=en" : "/"} className="justify-self-start">
        {c.home}
      </a>
    </section>
  );
}
```

📝 Tests (card P1-14, R-45): `src/components/booking/BookingForm.test.tsx` and `BookingThanks.test.tsx`. Use only
`fireEvent` from `@testing-library/react` (`@testing-library/user-event` is not installed; never add packages). Render
inside `<LanguageProvider initialLang="th">` (both components call `useLanguage()`, which throws without it). Use the
**real** `TurnstileWidget`: it skips Cloudflare's script when `window.turnstile` exists, so a fake API decides per test
whether a token arrives. Stub `ResizeObserver` in the test file (Radix radios inside a `<form>` need it and jsdom has
none; do not edit `src/test/setup.ts`). Known behaviour for the owner (not a bug to fix here): zod stops at an empty
age before the contact rule runs, so a parent who chose "call me back" without a number sees that message only after
choosing an age.

```tsx
import { fireEvent, render, screen, waitFor } from "@testing-library/react";
import { afterEach, beforeEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from "vitest";
import { bookingContent } from "@/content/booking";
import { LanguageProvider } from "@/i18n/LanguageProvider";
import { submitBookingRequest } from "@/lib/backend/booking.functions";
import { BookingForm } from "./BookingForm";

// useServerFn needs a router; the test calls the mocked server function directly instead.
vi.mock("@tanstack/react-start", async (importOriginal) => ({
  ...(await importOriginal<typeof import("@tanstack/react-start")>()),
  useServerFn: (fn: unknown) => fn,
}));
vi.mock("@/lib/backend/booking.functions", () => ({ submitBookingRequest: vi.fn() }));

class ResizeObserverStub {
  observe() {}
  unobserve() {}
  disconnect() {}
}

/** Fake Cloudflare API. emitToken = true gives a fake token at once, false never gives one. */
function fakeTurnstile(emitToken: boolean) {
  window.turnstile = {
    render: (_el, opts) => {
      if (emitToken) opts.callback("XXXX.DUMMY.TOKEN.XXXX");
      return "test-widget";
    },
    remove: () => {},
    reset: () => {},
  };
}

const c = bookingContent.th;

function setup(emitToken: boolean) {
  fakeTurnstile(emitToken);
  render(
    <LanguageProvider initialLang="th">
      <BookingForm initialPackage={null} onDone={vi.fn()} onFallback={vi.fn()} />
    </LanguageProvider>,
  );
}

describe("BookingForm", () => {
  beforeEach(() => vi.stubGlobal("ResizeObserver", ResizeObserverStub));
  afterEach(() => {
    vi.unstubAllGlobals();
    delete window.turnstile;
    vi.clearAllMocks();
  });

  it("keeps the honeypot away from people and keyboards", () => {
    setup(false);
    const trap = document.getElementById("company_website");
    expect(trap).toHaveAttribute("tabindex", "-1");
    expect(trap?.closest('[aria-hidden="true"]')).not.toBeNull();
  });
  it("has no default age", () => {
    setup(false);
    const age = screen.getByRole("combobox", { name: c.fields.child.age.label });
    expect((age as HTMLSelectElement).value).toBe("");
  });
  it("keeps submit disabled without a robot-check token", () => {
    setup(false);
    expect(screen.getByRole("button", { name: c.submit })).toBeDisabled();
  });
  it("gives every radio group an accessible name", () => {
    setup(false);
    for (const name of [
      c.fields.preferredContact.legend,
      c.fields.packageCode.legend,
      c.fields.preferredDay.legend,
    ]) {
      expect(screen.getByRole("radiogroup", { name })).toBeInTheDocument();
    }
  });
  it("links the short notice to /privacy (Thai page)", () => {
    setup(false);
    expect(screen.getByRole("link", { name: "นโยบายความเป็นส่วนตัว" })).toHaveAttribute(
      "href",
      "/privacy",
    );
  });
  it("asks for a phone number when 'call me back' has none", async () => {
    setup(true);
    fireEvent.change(screen.getByRole("textbox", { name: c.fields.guardianName.label }), {
      target: { value: "ผู้ปกครองทดสอบ" },
    });
    // choose an age first: an empty age stops zod before the contact rule runs
    fireEvent.change(screen.getByRole("combobox", { name: c.fields.child.age.label }), {
      target: { value: "7" },
    });
    fireEvent.click(screen.getByRole("radio", { name: c.fields.preferredContact.phone }));
    const send = screen.getByRole("button", { name: c.submit });
    await waitFor(() => expect(send).toBeEnabled());
    fireEvent.click(send);
    expect(await screen.findByText(c.fieldErrors.preferred_missing)).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(submitBookingRequest).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
  });
});
```

```tsx
import { render, screen } from "@testing-library/react";
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { bookingContent } from "@/content/booking";
import { LanguageProvider } from "@/i18n/LanguageProvider";
import { BookingThanks } from "./BookingThanks";

const c = bookingContent.th.thanks;

function setup() {
  render(
    <LanguageProvider initialLang="th">
      <BookingThanks refCode="CL-7KQ2M" />
    </LanguageProvider>,
  );
}

describe("BookingThanks", () => {
  it("moves focus to the heading and shows the code", () => {
    setup();
    expect(screen.getByRole("heading", { level: 1, name: c.title })).toHaveFocus();
    expect(screen.getByText("CL-7KQ2M")).toBeInTheDocument();
  });
  it("the LINE link carries the ref code and no personal data", () => {
    setup();
    const link = screen.getByRole("link", { name: new RegExp(c.lineButton) });
    const href = decodeURIComponent(link.getAttribute("href") ?? "");
    expect(href).toContain(c.lineMessage("CL-7KQ2M"));
    expect(href).not.toMatch(/0\d{8,9}/); // no phone-like number
  });
});
```

```tsx
import { createFileRoute } from "@tanstack/react-router";
import { useState } from "react";
import { BookingForm } from "@/components/booking/BookingForm";
import { BookingThanks } from "@/components/booking/BookingThanks";
import { ExternalLink } from "@/components/ExternalLink";
import { Footer } from "@/components/Footer";
import { LineIcon } from "@/components/icons";
import { SubpageHeader } from "@/components/SubpageHeader";
import { LINE_URL, lineMessageUrl } from "@/content/business";
import { bookingContent } from "@/content/booking";
import type { Lang } from "@/content/site";
import { LanguageProvider, useLanguage } from "@/i18n/LanguageProvider";
import type { PublicErrorCode } from "@/lib/backend/result";

type BookSearch = {
  lang?: Lang | undefined;
  package?: "single" | "four" | "eight" | undefined;
  session?: string | undefined;
};

export const Route = createFileRoute("/book")({
  validateSearch: (search: Record<string, unknown>): BookSearch => {
    const lang = search["lang"];
    const pkg = search["package"];
    const session = search["session"];
    return {
      lang: lang === "en" || lang === "th" ? lang : undefined,
      package: pkg === "single" || pkg === "four" || pkg === "eight" ? pkg : undefined,
      session:
        typeof session === "string" && /^[0-9a-f-]{36}$/i.test(session) ? session : undefined,
    };
  },
  head: () => ({
    meta: [
      { title: bookingContent.th.meta.title },
      { name: "description", content: bookingContent.th.meta.description },
    ],
  }),
  component: BookRoute,
});

function BookRoute() {
  const { lang } = Route.useSearch();
  return (
    <LanguageProvider initialLang={lang}>
      <BookPage />
    </LanguageProvider>
  );
}

type View =
  | { kind: "form" }
  | { kind: "thanks"; refCode: string }
  | { kind: "fallback"; code: PublicErrorCode };

function BookPage() {
  const { lang } = useLanguage();
  const search = Route.useSearch();
  const c = bookingContent[lang];
  const [view, setView] = useState<View>({ kind: "form" });

  return (
    <>
      <SubpageHeader />
      <main id="main" className="container-site max-w-[760px] py-10 md:py-16">
        {view.kind === "thanks" ? (
          <BookingThanks refCode={view.refCode} />
        ) : (
          <>
            <p className="eyebrow" lang="en">
              {c.intro.eyebrow}
            </p>
            <h1 className="mb-4 text-h2">{c.intro.title}</h1>
            <p className="section-lead mb-6">{c.intro.lead}</p>
            {/* LINE stays the primary, fastest channel */}
            <div className="mb-10 flex flex-wrap items-center gap-4 rounded-paper-alt bg-sage-soft p-5">
              <p className="m-0 flex-1 basis-60">{c.intro.lineFirst}</p>
              <ExternalLink href={LINE_URL} className="btn btn-primary">
                <LineIcon />
                {c.intro.lineButton}
              </ExternalLink>
            </div>
            {view.kind === "fallback" ? (
              <section
                role="alert"
                className="grid gap-4 rounded-paper border-2 border-ink bg-paper p-6"
              >
                <h2 className="text-h3">{c.fallback.title}</h2>
                <p>
                  {c.formErrors[view.code as keyof typeof c.formErrors] ??
                    c.formErrors.server_error}
                </p>
                <p>{c.fallback.body}</p>
                <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-3">
                  <ExternalLink
                    href={lineMessageUrl(c.fallback.lineMessage)}
                    className="btn btn-primary"
                  >
                    <LineIcon />
                    {c.fallback.lineButton}
                  </ExternalLink>
                  {view.code !== "form_closed" && view.code !== "rate_limited" && (
                    <button
                      type="button"
                      className="btn btn-ghost"
                      onClick={() => setView({ kind: "form" })}
                    >
                      {c.fallback.retry}
                    </button>
                  )}
                </div>
              </section>
            ) : (
              <BookingForm
                initialPackage={search.package ?? null}
                onDone={(refCode) => setView({ kind: "thanks", refCode })}
                onFallback={(code) => setView({ kind: "fallback", code })}
              />
            )}
          </>
        )}
      </main>
      <Footer hrefPrefix="/" />
    </>
  );
}
```

Analytics hook-in (F6-03, optional): add `data-umami-event="book-form-submit"` on the submit button and
`data-umami-event="book-line-click"` on the LINE buttons only when Umami is enabled (G12).

### 3.3 Open dates and seats left (F2-04) 📝

`src/components/booking/SessionPicker.tsx` (new name) shows open sessions **after render**, never blocking the page:

```tsx
const fetchOpen = useServerFn(getOpenSessions);
const q = useQuery({ queryKey: ["public", "open-sessions"], queryFn: () => fetchOpen(), staleTime: 60_000, retry: 1 });
// q.isPending → c.sessions.loading · q.isError or (q.data && !q.data.ok) → c.sessions.unavailable
// q.data.ok && q.data.data.length === 0 → c.sessions.none
```

- A `RadioGroup` with one card per session: `formatSessionDate(date, lang)` + "09:00–15:00" + a badge:
  `seatsLeft === null` → "เปิดรับ / Open"; `> 0` → "ว่าง 3 ที่ / 3 seats left"; `0` → "เต็มแล้ว / Full" plus
  `waitlistNote` when selected (full dates stay selectable; the copy makes no promise about seats freeing up, R-24).
  Show `publicNoteTh` / `publicNoteEn` under the date when present.
- First option "ยังไม่เลือกวัน / No date yet" (value `""` → `sessionId: null`).
- Choosing a session sets `sessionId` and `preferredDate = sessionDate`, and sets `preferredDay` from the weekday.
- `?session=<uuid>` preselects it when that id is in the list.
- Never show capacity, other families, or names. Badges are text, not colour only.

Optional homepage strip (`src/components/sections/OpenDates.tsx`, new name), shown under the pricing title: the next
4 open dates as chips linking to `/book?session=<id>` (+ `&lang=en`). Hidden entirely while loading, on error, or
when empty (the homepage never shows an error). Add to `SiteContent.pricing`:

| Key | TH | EN |
|---|---|---|
| `openDates.title` | รอบที่เปิดรับเร็วๆ นี้ | Upcoming open dates |
| `openDates.seatsLeft(n)` | ว่าง {n} ที่ | {n} seats left (1 → "1 seat left") |
| `openDates.open` | เปิดรับ | Open |
| `openDates.full` | เต็มแล้ว | Full |
| `openDates.more` | ดูวันอื่นและฝากข้อมูล | See more dates and leave your details |

### 3.4 `/privacy` (F1-02 `…-v1`, F3-08 `…-v2`, F4-10 `…-v3`) ✅ (rev)

**Rev 14 Oct 2026 (what the notice must say; the lawyer reviews every version, G9a):**
- **v2 (Stage B):** add the optional news-and-classes paragraph (`privacy.marketing` in § 5.2), stating that it is guardian-level,
  optional, separate from every other choice, and says truthfully what child information it uses: only the child's nickname, in
  the studio's internal follow-up list (decision 4, round two; lawyer review G9a and G9b). It must not say "never uses children's data".
- **v3 (Stage C):** add the print channel (`privacy.print` in § 5.2) to the channel list, and say that every channel, including
  marketing, can be changed on `/my`.
- The absolute website in the notice is `https://cherryleeandco.com` (decision 9). The JSON-LD change is a website task, not part of this plan.


> **Single source (R-14, R-02, R-28).** The **wording** of the notice lives only in `50-security-privacy.md` § 11.3 (TH)
> and § 11.4 (EN). This section is only the **code pattern** for `src/content/privacy.tsx` and the route. Build rules:
> 1. Keep the types, constants, `meta`, `title`, `updated`, `draftBanner`, `summaryTitle`, `summary`, `tocLabel` and the
>    route code below as written (set the month in `updated` to the month in `PRIVACY_NOTICE_VERSION`).
> 2. Build each language's `sections` array from 50 §§ 11.3/11.4: **one `PrivacySection` per row of the table below, in
>    this order**. `title` = the 50 heading text without its number or `[Pn]` tag; `body` = that heading's text as JSX
>    (`<p>`, `<ul>`, and a `<table>` for the two tables, with `<Nobr>`/`<En>` per AGENTS.md). A `{{…}}` placeholder
>    becomes the matching constant (`CONTROLLER_LEGAL_NAME`, `EMAIL`, `LINE_ID`) or the visible text
>    `[owner to fill: …]`, which G9a removes. The code block below holds **one** sample section per language
>    (`controller`, copied from 50 heading 2) to show the JSX style; every other section is built the same way from 50.
> 3. Inside a section, a row or paragraph tagged with a later phase (`[P3]`, `[P4]`, `[P5]`, `[P6]`) is left out until
>    that phase's notice card adds it. Remove the `[Pn]` tags and HTML comments from the built text.
> 4. Every section object has `phase` (the stage that adds it): `export type PrivacySection = { id: string; phase: "P1"
>    | "P3" | "P4" | "P5" | "P6"; title: string; body: ReactNode };`. Version format `YYYY-MM-vN` only (e.g.
>    `"2026-11-v1"`), bumped by one in each stage's notice card.
>
> | # | 50 heading (§ 11.3 / § 11.4) | `id` | `phase` | Added by card |
> |---|---|---|---|---|
> | 1 | 1 About this notice | `about` | P1 | P1-02 |
> | 2 | 2 Who looks after your data | `controller` | P1 | P1-02 |
> | 3 | 3.1 Visiting the website | `website` | P1 | P1-02 |
> | 4 | 3.2 The booking form | `booking-form` | P1 | P1-02 |
> | 5 | 3.3 Chatting with us | `chat` | P1 | P1-02 |
> | 6 | 3.4 Family records | `family-records` | P3 | P3-16 |
> | 7 | 3.5 Emergency contacts and pickup | `contacts` | P3 | P3-16 |
> | 8 | 3.6 Allergy and care information | `health` | P4 | P4-20, only if G-HEALTH = system |
> | 9 | 3.7 Photos and videos | `media` | P4 | P4-20 |
> | 10 | 3.8 Private family links (incl. the `[P5]` `/my` sentence) | `links` | P4 | P4-20 |
> | 11 | 3.9 Rights requests and incident records | `rights-records` | P1 | P1-02 (R-28: the owner's runbook log exists from Stage A) |
> | 12 | 4 Children's information | `children` | P1 | P1-02; its `[P4]` paragraph: P4-20 |
> | 13 | 5 Who we share data with | `sharing` | P1 | P1-02; `[P3]` rows: P3-16; `[P4]` row: P4-20; `[P6]` row: parked |
> | 14 | 6 Data stored outside Thailand | `abroad` | P1 | P1-02 |
> | 15 | 7 How long we keep data | `retention` | P1 | P1-02; `[P3]` rows: P3-16; `[P4]` rows: P4-20 |
> | 16 | 8 How we keep data safe | `security` | P1 | P1-02 |
> | 17 | 9 Your rights | `rights` | P1 | P1-02 |
> | 18 | 10 Cookies and similar technologies | `cookies` | P1 | P1-02; `[P6]` line: parked |
> | 19 | 11 Changes to this notice | `changes` | P1 | P1-02 |
> | 20 | 12 Contact us | `contact` | P1 | P1-02 |

Files: `src/routes/privacy.tsx`, `src/content/privacy.tsx`. Layered notice: a short "In short" box first, then a table
of contents and full sections (PDPA s.23 items). Linked from the `/book` notice, the enrol and consent pages, and the
footer (add `t.subpage.privacyLink` as a link in `Footer` next to the credit line).

Before go-live (G9a): the owner fills `CONTROLLER_LEGAL_NAME` (legal form, open question 1), a lawyer reviews the
text, then `draftBanner` is set to `null` in both languages. Every change of meaning bumps
`PRIVACY_NOTICE_VERSION` (format `YYYY-MM-vN`); `/book` stores the version each parent saw. Stage A ships `…-v1`
(`[P1]`), Stage B `…-v2` (+ `[P3]`), Stage C `…-v3` (+ `[P4]` and the `/my` part of `[P5]`), always **before** the stage
goes live (G9a, 00 § 8.0).

```tsx
import type { ReactNode } from "react";
import { EMAIL, LINE_ID } from "./business";
import { En, Nobr, type Lang } from "./site";

/**
 * Privacy notice (PDPA s.23), Thai + English. Wording source: 50-security-privacy.md §§ 11.3–11.4.
 * …-v1 (F1-02): [P1] sections. …-v2 (F3-08): + [P3]. …-v3 (F4-10): + [P4] and the /my part of [P5].
 *
 * DRAFT until gate G9a: the owner approves it and a Thai PDPA lawyer reviews it. This is not legal advice.
 * Bump PRIVACY_NOTICE_VERSION whenever the meaning changes; /book stores the version a parent saw.
 */
export const PRIVACY_NOTICE_VERSION = "2026-10-v1";

/**
 * Filled in by the owner at G0 (legal form: sole proprietor / partnership / company).
 * Until then the page shows the trading name only.
 */
export const CONTROLLER_LEGAL_NAME: string | null = null;

export type PrivacySection = {
  id: string;
  /** Stage that adds this section (R-02, R-28) */
  phase: "P1" | "P3" | "P4" | "P5" | "P6";
  title: string;
  body: ReactNode;
};
export type PrivacyContent = {
  meta: { title: string; description: string };
  title: string;
  updated: string;
  /** Shown on the page until G9a is done; set to null after approval */
  draftBanner: string | null;
  summaryTitle: string;
  summary: ReactNode[];
  tocLabel: string;
  sections: PrivacySection[];
};

/** Used by the `rights` section (50 § 11.3/11.4 heading 9). */
const PDPC_COMPLAINTS = "https://complaint.pdpc.or.th/";

  draftBanner: "ฉบับร่าง รอเจ้าของสตูดิโอและนักกฎหมายตรวจทานก่อนเผยแพร่",
  summaryTitle: "สรุปสั้นๆ",
  summary: [
    "เราขอข้อมูลเท่าที่จำเป็นเพื่อติดต่อกลับเรื่องการเรียนเท่านั้น",
    "เราไม่ขาย ไม่ให้เช่า และไม่ใช้ข้อมูลของคุณเพื่อโฆษณา",
    "เว็บไซต์นี้ไม่ใช้คุกกี้ติดตามหรือโฆษณา",
    "ถ้ายังไม่ได้เริ่มเรียน เราลบข้อมูลคำขอภายใน 6 เดือน",
    <>
      อยากดู แก้ไข หรือลบข้อมูล ทักเราทาง <En>LINE</En> อีเมล หรือมาที่สตูดิโอได้เลยค่ะ
    </>,
  ],
  tocLabel: "หัวข้อในหน้านี้",
  // ONE sample section shows the JSX style. Build the full array from 50 § 11.3, one entry per row of the
  // id/phase table above, in that order (rule 2). Wording comes only from 50; never from this sample.
  sections: [
    {
      id: "controller",
      phase: "P1",
      title: "ใครดูแลข้อมูลของคุณ",
      body: (
        <ul>
          <li>
            <Nobr>ผู้ควบคุมข้อมูลส่วนบุคคล</Nobr>:{" "}
            {CONTROLLER_LEGAL_NAME ?? "[owner to fill: CONTROLLER_NAME_TH]"}
          </li>

const en: PrivacyContent = {

    description:

          </li>
          <li>Privacy contact: [owner to fill: PRIVACY_CONTACT_EN]</li>
        </ul>
      ),

};

export const privacyContent: Record<Lang, PrivacyContent> = { th, en };
```

✅ `src/routes/privacy.tsx`:

```tsx
import { createFileRoute } from "@tanstack/react-router";
import { Footer } from "@/components/Footer";
import { SubpageHeader } from "@/components/SubpageHeader";
import { privacyContent } from "@/content/privacy";
import type { Lang } from "@/content/site";
import { LanguageProvider, useLanguage } from "@/i18n/LanguageProvider";

export const Route = createFileRoute("/privacy")({
  validateSearch: (search: Record<string, unknown>): { lang?: Lang | undefined } => {
    const lang = search["lang"];
    return { lang: lang === "en" || lang === "th" ? lang : undefined };
  },
  head: () => ({
    meta: [
      { title: privacyContent.th.meta.title },
      { name: "description", content: privacyContent.th.meta.description },
    ],
  }),
  component: PrivacyRoute,
});

function PrivacyRoute() {
  const { lang } = Route.useSearch();
  return (
    <LanguageProvider initialLang={lang}>
      <PrivacyPage />
    </LanguageProvider>
  );
}

function PrivacyPage() {
  const { lang } = useLanguage();
  const p = privacyContent[lang];
  return (
    <>
      <SubpageHeader />
      <main id="main" className="container-site max-w-[760px] py-10 md:py-16">
        {p.draftBanner && (
          <p className="mb-6 rounded-xl border-2 border-mustard-ink bg-mustard-soft px-4 py-3 font-medium text-ink">
            {p.draftBanner}
          </p>
        )}
        <h1 className="mb-2 text-h2">{p.title}</h1>
        <p className="mb-8 text-sm text-ink-soft">{p.updated}</p>
        <section
          aria-labelledby="summary-title"
          className="mb-10 rounded-paper-alt bg-sage-soft p-6"
        >
          <h2 id="summary-title" className="mb-3 text-h3">
            {p.summaryTitle}
          </h2>
          <ul role="list" className="grid list-disc gap-2 pl-5">
            {p.summary.map((s, i) => (
              <li key={i}>{s}</li>
            ))}
          </ul>
        </section>
        <nav aria-label={p.tocLabel} className="mb-10">
          <ol className="grid list-decimal gap-1 pl-6">
            {p.sections.map((s) => (
              <li key={s.id}>
                <a href={`#${s.id}`} className="inline-flex min-h-11 items-center">
                  {s.title}
                </a>
              </li>
            ))}
          </ol>
        </nav>
        {p.sections.map((s) => (
          <section
            key={s.id}
            id={s.id}
            aria-labelledby={`${s.id}-title`}
            className="privacy-prose mb-10"
          >
            <h2 id={`${s.id}-title`} className="mb-3 text-h3">
              {s.title}
            </h2>
            {s.body}
          </section>
        ))}
      </main>
      <Footer hrefPrefix="/" />
    </>
  );
}
```
