### 3.5 `/enrol/$token` (F4-04)

Route file `src/routes/enrol.$token.tsx`:

```tsx
export const Route = createFileRoute("/enrol/$token")({
  ssr: false, // private link: nothing rendered or cached on the server
  head: () => ({
    meta: [
      { name: "robots", content: "noindex, nofollow" },
      { name: "referrer", content: "no-referrer" },
      { title: enrolContent.th.meta.title },
    ],
  }),
  validateSearch: (s: Record<string, unknown>): { lang?: Lang | undefined } => ({
    lang: s["lang"] === "en" || s["lang"] === "th" ? s["lang"] : undefined,
  }),
  component: EnrolRoute, // <LanguageProvider initialLang={lang}><EnrolPage token={token} /></LanguageProvider>
});
```

(✅ the `ssr: false` + `head` part compiled.) Data: `getEnrolmentForm({ data: { token, lang } })` on mount (POST; keep
the result in component state, **not** React Query, so the token isn't part of a cache key). Re-fetch when the
language changes (the consent texts are per language).

States: loading → `linkProblem` card (`link_invalid` / `link_expired` / `link_used` / `server_error`, each with the
LINE button) → the form → submitting → `done` (focus on heading; no data shown back).

Page order (00-overview § 6 stage 3), one column, a simple step list at the top (`steps`, not clickable):

1. **Guardian**: full name, phone, LINE ID (at least one), optional email (hint: receipts only), and the required
   checkbox **"ฉันเป็นพ่อ แม่ หรือผู้ใช้อำนาจปกครอง…"** (`confirmsParentalResponsibility: true`).
2. **For each child** (cards titled "น้อง{nickname}"):
   - Birth month and year: two native selects (month names from `child.months`; years from `today − 13` to
     `today − 5`, labelled with `child.yearLabel` (Thai shows พ.ศ.), value CE) → `birthMonth: "YYYY-MM"`.
     After a choice, if `needsChildConsent(birthMonth, todayInBangkok())` show `child.olderChildNote`.
   - **Health** (`HealthSection` below; shown only when `getEnrolmentForm` returns a current `health_allergy` text. With
     G-HEALTH = paper the builder removes the health rows from the unapplied 0006, so there is none, R-29. When the
     child's own latest health decision is a "no", `childRefusedHealth` is true and the section shows only
     `health.childRefusedNote`, submitted as `skipped`, R-34): its own unticked
     explicit-consent checkbox; the checkbox **label is `consent_texts.title`** and the full `body_md` is shown right
     above it (that exact text is the evidence). Two toggle buttons (`aria-pressed`), R-07:
     **"ไม่ใช่ตอนนี้ / Not now"** → nothing recorded, nothing deleted (submitted as `skipped`), warm note;
     **"ไม่ต้องการให้ข้อมูลนี้ / I don't want to share this"** → submitted as `refused` (the server records `withdrawn`
     if the family had agreed before), with `health.declineNote` saying any allergy info shared earlier will be
     deleted. Neither used → `skipped`.
   - **Media** (`MediaSection`, R-04): lead text, then **one block per purpose whose text is published** (in
     `MEDIA_PURPOSES` order). Each block shows, **directly above its control and never behind a link or collapsible**,
     the `consent_texts.title` as a heading and the full `body_md` (`ConsentTextBody`), then its own **unticked**
     `Checkbox` labelled `media.agree` ("ยินยอมตามข้อความด้านบน / I agree to the text above"). Purposes with no
     published text are not shown and nothing is sent for them (R-06; show `media.notReady` once if none is
     published). If any box is ticked, a required `RadioGroup` (no default): `faceVisible` / `faceHidden`
     (`media_level_required`). Note for 10+ (`media.olderChildNote`). On a link re-sent to an enrolled family, a
     purpose the guardian already decided shows its current decision and `media.keepNote`; its box still starts
     unticked and, left unticked, changes nothing (R-33).
3. **Contacts** (0–4, `useFieldArray`): name, phone, relationship (hint), roles (two checkboxes: emergency /
   may collect, at least one: `role_required`), and the required **"ฉันแจ้งบุคคลนี้แล้ว…"** checkbox
   (`informed_required`). Prefilled from existing contacts (keep their `id`). `noIdNote` under the heading.
4. **Review & send**: short lead, link to `/privacy`, submit.

Submit mapping (browser): build `SubmitEnrolmentRaw` from the form; per child
`health: unset or not_now → { decision: "skipped" }`, `refused → { decision: "refused", consentTextId }`,
`granted → { decision: "granted", consentTextId, allergens, otherAllergens, foodsToAvoid, careNotes }`;
`media` = one `{ purpose, on, consentTextId }` per purpose **shown** (absent purposes are not sent, R-06; for an
already decided purpose `on: false` means "no change", R-33). Run `SubmitEnrolmentInput.safeParse` before sending and show field
errors (`enrolContent.fieldErrors`) the same way as `/book`. Server results: `ok` → done; `invalid_input` →
`formErrors.invalid_input` (suggest reload); `link_*` → the link-problem card; `server_error` → message, keep values.

✅ (rev) `src/components/enrol/HealthSection.tsx` (R-07: "Not now" records nothing; a separate decline records a refusal;
R-34: optional `childRefused` replaces the whole box with `health.childRefusedNote`):

```tsx
import { Checkbox } from "@/components/ui/checkbox";
import { Input } from "@/components/ui/input";
import { Textarea } from "@/components/ui/textarea";
import { enrolContent } from "@/content/enrol";
import { useLanguage } from "@/i18n/LanguageProvider";
import type { ConsentTextView } from "@/lib/backend/enrolment.schema";
import { ALLERGENS, type Allergen } from "@/lib/backend/health.schema";
import { ALLERGEN_SCREEN_GROUPS } from "@/components/health/allergen-groups";

// src/components/health/allergen-groups.ts (round two, decisions 7 and 8, 14 Oct 2026). Browser-safe, no secrets.
// Screen order. Every key in ALLERGENS appears exactly once. The nut keys are one group on screen ("ถั่ว" / "Nuts"),
// and the data keeps both keys (peanut, tree_nut). Flour and wheat are separate rows.
export const ALLERGEN_SCREEN_GROUPS: ReadonlyArray<ReadonlyArray<Allergen>> = [
  ["peanut", "tree_nut"],
  ["milk"],
  ["egg"],
  ["wheat"],
  ["flour"],
  ["soy"],
  ["fish"],
  ["shellfish"],
  ["sesame"],
];
import { ConsentTextBody } from "./ConsentTextBody";

/**
 * UI state for one child. "unset" (nothing used) and "not_now" are both submitted as decision "skipped":
 * nothing recorded, nothing deleted. Only "refused" (the explicit decline button) records a refusal (R-07).
 */
export type HealthUi =
  | { decision: "unset" }
  | { decision: "not_now" }
  | { decision: "refused" }
  | {
      decision: "granted";
      allergens: Allergen[];
      otherAllergens: string;
      foodsToAvoid: string;
      careNotes: string;
    };

export const EMPTY_GRANTED: HealthUi = {
  decision: "granted",
  allergens: [],
  otherAllergens: "",
  foodsToAvoid: "",
  careNotes: "",
};

type Props = {
  childKey: string; // unique per child (use childId), keeps ids unique
  text: ConsentTextView | null; // current health_allergy text in the page language
  showOlderChildNote: boolean;
  value: HealthUi;
  onChange: (next: HealthUi) => void;
  /** R-34: EnrolmentForm.children[].childRefusedHealth. true = no box at all; the value stays "unset" (= skipped) */
  childRefused?: boolean;
};

/**
 * PDPA s.26: explicit consent, its own UNTICKED checkbox, separate from everything else, with a warm
 * "Not now". Fields appear only after the box is ticked. Never pre-tick, never default to "granted".
 */
export function HealthSection({
  childKey,
  text,
  showOlderChildNote,
  value,
  onChange,
  childRefused = false,
}: Props) {
  const { lang } = useLanguage();
  const c = enrolContent[lang].health;
  const id = (s: string) => `health-${childKey}-${s}`;

  if (!text) return <p className="text-ink-soft">{c.notReady}</p>;
  if (childRefused) {
    return (
      <fieldset className="grid gap-2">
        <legend className="mb-1 font-display text-h3">{c.title}</legend>
        <p className="rounded-xl bg-sage-soft p-3">{c.childRefusedNote}</p>
      </fieldset>
    );
  }

  const granted = value.decision === "granted" ? value : null;
  const toggleAllergen = (a: Allergen, on: boolean) => {
    if (!granted) return;
    const set = new Set(granted.allergens);
    if (on) set.add(a);
    else set.delete(a);
    onChange({ ...granted, allergens: ALLERGENS.filter((x) => set.has(x)) });
  };

  return (
    <fieldset className="grid gap-4">
      <legend className="mb-1 font-display text-h3">{c.title}</legend>
      <p>{c.lead}</p>

      <div className="grid gap-3 rounded-2xl border-2 border-ink bg-paper p-4">
        <p className="font-semibold">{c.consentBoxHeading}</p>
        <ConsentTextBody markdown={text.bodyMd} />
        <label htmlFor={id("consent")} className="flex min-h-11 cursor-pointer items-start gap-3">
          <Checkbox
            id={id("consent")}
            className="mt-1 h-6 w-6"
            checked={granted !== null}
            onCheckedChange={(checked) =>
              onChange(checked === true ? EMPTY_GRANTED : { decision: "unset" })
            }
          />
          <span className="font-medium">{text.title}</span>
        </label>
        <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2">
          <button
            type="button"
            aria-pressed={value.decision === "not_now"}
            onClick={() =>
              onChange(value.decision === "not_now" ? { decision: "unset" } : { decision: "not_now" })
            }
            className="btn btn-ghost btn-sm"
          >
            {value.decision === "not_now" ? c.noThanksPressed : c.noThanks}
          </button>
          <button
            type="button"
            aria-pressed={value.decision === "refused"}
            onClick={() =>
              onChange(value.decision === "refused" ? { decision: "unset" } : { decision: "refused" })
            }
            className="btn btn-ghost btn-sm"
          >
            {value.decision === "refused" ? c.declinePressed : c.decline}
          </button>
        </div>
        <div aria-live="polite">
          {value.decision === "not_now" ? (
            <p className="rounded-xl bg-sage-soft p-3">{c.noThanksNote}</p>
          ) : null}
          {value.decision === "refused" ? (
            <p className="rounded-xl bg-sage-soft p-3">{c.declineNote}</p>
          ) : null}
        </div>
      </div>

      {granted && (
        <div className="grid gap-4">
          <p className="text-sm text-ink-soft">{c.replaceNote}</p>
          <fieldset>
            <legend className="mb-2 font-medium">{c.allergensLegend}</legend>
            {/* Round two (14 Oct 2026): grouped by ALLERGEN_SCREEN_GROUPS (below). peanut and tree_nut share one fieldset "ถั่ว" / "Nuts"
                with two checkboxes; the two keys stay separate in the data. renderRow(a) is the label + Checkbox markup above. */}
            <ul role="list" className="grid gap-1 sm:grid-cols-2">
              {ALLERGEN_SCREEN_GROUPS.map((group) =>
                group.length === 1 ? (
                  <li key={group[0]}>{renderRow(group[0])}</li>
                ) : (
                  <li key={group.join("+")} className="sm:col-span-2">
                    <fieldset>
                      <legend className="mt-2 font-medium">{c.allergensNutsGroup}</legend>
                      <ul role="list" className="grid gap-1 sm:grid-cols-2">
                        {group.map((a) => (
                          <li key={a}>{renderRow(a)}</li>
                        ))}
                      </ul>
                    </fieldset>
                  </li>
                ),
              )}
            </ul>
          </fieldset>
          <div className="grid gap-1.5">
            <label htmlFor={id("other")} className="font-medium">
              {c.otherAllergens.label}
            </label>
            <Input
              id={id("other")}
              maxLength={200}
              aria-describedby={id("other-hint")}
              value={granted.otherAllergens}
              onChange={(e) => onChange({ ...granted, otherAllergens: e.target.value })}
              className="min-h-11 bg-paper text-base"
            />
            <p id={id("other-hint")} className="text-sm text-ink-soft">
              {c.otherAllergens.hint}
            </p>
          </div>
          <div className="grid gap-1.5">
            <label htmlFor={id("avoid")} className="font-medium">
              {c.foodsToAvoid.label}
            </label>
            <Textarea
              id={id("avoid")}
              maxLength={300}
              rows={2}
              aria-describedby={id("avoid-hint")}
              value={granted.foodsToAvoid}
              onChange={(e) => onChange({ ...granted, foodsToAvoid: e.target.value })}
              className="bg-paper text-base"
            />
            <p id={id("avoid-hint")} className="text-sm text-ink-soft">
              {c.foodsToAvoid.hint}
            </p>
          </div>
          <div className="grid gap-1.5">
            <label htmlFor={id("care")} className="font-medium">
              {c.careNotes.label}
            </label>
            <Textarea
              id={id("care")}
              maxLength={500}
              rows={3}
              aria-describedby={id("care-hint")}
              value={granted.careNotes}
              onChange={(e) => onChange({ ...granted, careNotes: e.target.value })}
              className="bg-paper text-base"
            />
            <p id={id("care-hint")} className="text-sm text-ink-soft">
              {c.careNotes.hint}
            </p>
          </div>
          {showOlderChildNote ? <p className="text-sm text-ink-soft">{c.olderChildNote}</p> : null}
        </div>
      )}
    </fieldset>
  );
}
```

✅ `src/components/enrol/ConsentTextBody.tsx` (renders stored wording as text, never as HTML):

```tsx
/**
 * Renders consent_texts.body_md as plain paragraphs and "- " bullet lists. No HTML, no markdown
 * library, no dangerouslySetInnerHTML: the text is shown exactly as stored (it is evidence, D12).
 */
export function ConsentTextBody({ markdown }: { markdown: string }) {
  const blocks = markdown.trim().split(/\n\s*\n/);
  return (
    <div className="grid gap-2 text-[0.95rem] leading-relaxed">
      {blocks.map((block, i) => {
        const lines = block.split("\n").map((l) => l.trim());
        if (lines.every((l) => l.startsWith("- "))) {
          return (
            <ul key={i} className="grid list-disc gap-1 pl-5">
              {lines.map((l, j) => (
                <li key={j}>{l.slice(2)}</li>
              ))}
            </ul>
          );
        }
        return <p key={i}>{lines.join(" ")}</p>;
      })}
    </div>
  );
}
```

✅ (rev) Test that guards the most important rule (no pre-ticked consent), `HealthSection.test.tsx` (rev: + the R-34 case):

```tsx
import { fireEvent, render, screen } from "@testing-library/react";
import { LanguageProvider } from "@/i18n/LanguageProvider";
import { HealthSection, type HealthUi } from "./HealthSection";

const text = {
  id: "00000000-0000-4000-8000-000000000001",
  version: 1,
  title: "ยินยอมโดยชัดแจ้ง (ทดสอบ)",
  bodyMd: "ข้อความทดสอบ",
};

function setup(value: HealthUi = { decision: "unset" }) {
  const onChange = vi.fn();
  render(
    <LanguageProvider initialLang="th">
      <HealthSection
        childKey="c1"
        text={text}
        showOlderChildNote={false}
        value={value}
        onChange={onChange}
      />
    </LanguageProvider>,
  );
  return onChange;
}

describe("HealthSection", () => {
  it("starts with the consent box NOT ticked and no health fields", () => {
    setup();
    expect(screen.getByRole("checkbox", { name: text.title })).not.toBeChecked();
    expect(screen.queryByLabelText(/ถั่วลิสง/)).toBeNull();
  });
  it("ticking the box is the only way to reach the fields", () => {
    const onChange = setup();
    fireEvent.click(screen.getByRole("checkbox", { name: text.title }));
    expect(onChange).toHaveBeenCalledWith(expect.objectContaining({ decision: "granted" }));
  });
  it("'Not now' records nothing (R-07)", () => {
    const onChange = setup();
    fireEvent.click(screen.getByRole("button", { name: "ไม่ใช่ตอนนี้" }));
    expect(onChange).toHaveBeenCalledWith({ decision: "not_now" });
  });
  it("the explicit decline is a separate button", () => {
    const onChange = setup();
    fireEvent.click(screen.getByRole("button", { name: "ไม่ต้องการให้ข้อมูลนี้" }));
    expect(onChange).toHaveBeenCalledWith({ decision: "refused" });
  });
  it("shows the full consent text in the DOM, not hidden (R-04)", () => {
    setup();
    expect(screen.getByText("ข้อความทดสอบ")).toBeVisible();
  });
  it("after the child's own 'no' shows no consent box at all (R-34)", () => {
    render(
      <LanguageProvider initialLang="th">
        <HealthSection
          childKey="c2"
          text={text}
          showOlderChildNote
          value={{ decision: "unset" }}
          onChange={vi.fn()}
          childRefused
        />
      </LanguageProvider>,
    );
    expect(screen.queryByRole("checkbox")).toBeNull();
    expect(screen.queryByText("ข้อความทดสอบ")).toBeNull();
  });
});
```

Partial<Record<MediaPurpose, ConsentTextView>>; current: Partial<Record<MediaPurpose, CurrentMediaDecision>>; value: {
on: Partial<Record<MediaPurpose, boolean>>; level: "face_visible" | "face_hidden" | null }; showOlderChildNote: boolean;
onChange(next) }` (`current` = `EnrolmentForm.children[].currentMedia`, § 9.8 of `20-server-api.md`). For each purpose
in `MEDIA_PURPOSES` **that has a text**: a bordered block with `<h4>{text.title}</h4>`,
`<ConsentTextBody markdown={text.bodyMd} />`; when `current[p]` exists, a status line under the text
(`media.currentGranted(formatDateTime(decidedAt, lang))` for `granted`, else `media.currentNotGranted(…)`); then a
`<label>` row (min-h-11) with an unticked `Checkbox` (`checked={value.on[p] === true}`, never `defaultChecked`, never
pre-ticked from `current`) and `media.agree`; when `current[p]` exists, `media.keepNote` under the box (an unticked
box keeps the earlier decision, R-33). No `Collapsible`, no "read more". Then the face-level `RadioGroup` (no default
value) only when any box is ticked. Tests (`MediaSection.test.tsx`): every checkbox starts unchecked, **also** when
`current` says `granted`; every published purpose's `bodyMd` is visible in the DOM (`toBeVisible`); a purpose without a
text renders nothing; ticking one shows the face-level question with no option selected; a purpose with
`current: { decision: "granted", decidedAt }` shows the status line and `keepNote`.

```tsx
import type { ReactNode } from "react";
import { En, Nobr, type Lang } from "./site";
import type { Allergen } from "@/lib/backend/health.schema";
import type { MediaPurpose } from "@/lib/backend/common.schema";
import type { PublicErrorCode } from "@/lib/backend/result";

/**
 * Copy for the private enrolment link /enrol/$token (F4-04). Parents fill this in before the first class.
 * The consent WORDING itself (health + each media channel) comes from consent_texts in the database,
 * so the exact text shown is provable (D12). This file holds everything around it.
 * Never pre-tick a box; never combine two purposes in one control.
 */

export type EnrolFieldErrorCode =
  | "required"
  | "too_long"
  | "invalid_phone"
  | "invalid_line_id"
  | "invalid_email"
  | "contact_required"
  | "responsibility_required"
  | "birth_month_required"
  | "media_level_required"
  | "role_required"
  | "informed_required"
  | "invalid";

export type EnrolContent = {
  meta: { title: string };
  loading: string;
  linkProblem: {
    title: Record<"link_invalid" | "link_expired" | "link_used" | "server_error", string>;
    body: Record<"link_invalid" | "link_expired" | "link_used" | "server_error", ReactNode>;
    lineButton: string;
    lineMessage: string;
  };
  intro: {
    title: string;
    hello: (familyName: string) => string;
    lead: ReactNode;
    time: string;
    expires: (date: string) => string;
    privacy: ReactNode;
  };
  steps: [string, string, string, string, string];
  guardian: {
    title: string;
    lead: string;
    fullName: string;
    phone: string;
    lineId: string;
    email: { label: string; hint: string };
    responsibility: { label: string; hint: string };
  };
  child: {
    title: (nickname: string) => string;
    birthMonth: { legend: string; month: string; year: string; hint: string };
    months: string[];
    /** Thai shows the Buddhist year; the value stays CE */

    title: string;
    lead: ReactNode;
    notReady: string;
    consentBoxHeading: string;

    declinePressed: string;

    foodsToAvoid: { label: string; hint: string };
    careNotes: { label: string; hint: string };

    faceVisible: string;
    faceHidden: string;
    levelHint: string;
    olderChildNote: string;
    notReady: string;

    pickup: string;

    add: string;

  };
  review: { title: string; lead: string; submit: string; submitting: string };
  errorSummary: (n: number) => string;

  formErrors: Record<

    >,

  >;

};

    submitting: "Sending…",
  },
  errorSummary: (n) => (n === 1 ? "1 field needs a fix." : `${n} fields need a fix.`),
  fieldErrors: {
    required: "Please fill this in.",
    too_long: "That’s a bit too long.",
    invalid_phone: "That phone number doesn’t look right.",

    invalid_email: "That email address doesn’t look right.",

  formErrors: {

      "Some details aren’t right, or a consent text was just updated. Please reload this page.",
    server_error: "Sorry, something went wrong on our side and nothing was sent. Please try again.",

    manageNote:
      "You can change your consent choices at any time. Just ask us on LINE for your consent link.",
    home: "Back to home",

};

```

### 3.6 `/my/$token` (F4-09, moved from P5 to Stage C; was F5-03) ✅ (rev)

Route file `src/routes/my.$token.tsx`: same `ssr: false`, `noindex`, `no-referrer`, `?lang=` handling as § 3.5.
Data: `getMyConsents({ data: { token, lang } })` into component state. Layout:

- `h1` "ความยินยอมของฉัน", `hello(familyName)`, lead.
- One card per child, **every child of the family** (R-27); a child with `isActive = false` gets the label
  `inactiveChild` but keeps every withdraw button. Inside, one row per purpose (5 rows, health first; a purpose with
  no published text and no recorded decision is not shown, so with G-HEALTH = paper (no health text, R-29) the health
  row only appears for a decision recorded earlier). Each row: purpose `name` + `what`; status
  text (not colour only): `granted` (+ `grantedFaceHidden` when `mediaLevel = face_hidden`), `refused`,
  `withdrawn`, `none`; when `status = needs_child` also show `state.needsChild`; `since(date)` with
  `formatSessionDate`-style date (Thai Buddhist year).
- Actions (buttons ≥ 44 px):
  - Granted → **"ถอนความยินยอม / Withdraw consent"**: one press calls `updateMyConsent({ decision: "withdrawn" })`
    immediately (no confirm dialog: withdrawing must be as easy as giving, PDPA s.19). Then show `afterWithdraw.media`
    or `afterWithdraw.health` in an `aria-live="polite"` region under the row.
  - Not granted, media → **"ให้ความยินยอม / Give consent"** opens a small `Dialog` with the purpose `title` and its
    full text (`ConsentTextBody`, visible, never collapsed), an unticked `Checkbox` `media.agree`-style
    ("ยินยอมตามข้อความด้านบน / I agree to the text above", required), the face choice (required, no default) and
    "ยืนยัน / Confirm".
  - Not granted, health → no button; show `healthGrantNote` (ask for an enrol link; never send health in chat).
- Each row shows the purpose's current consent text **in full** under the status (R-04), so the parent always sees
  what they agreed to; no collapsible.
- `otherRights` box at the bottom (address, registered mail, 30 days).
- Errors: `errors[code]` in an `aria-live` region next to the row; `link_*` → link-problem card.

**Rev 14 Oct 2026: guardian card (P4-22; decision 2).** Below the children cards, one card for the family's primary guardian,
titled `my.guardianMarketing.title`. It uses `MarketingConsentBlock` (§ 2.6) in `manage` mode with `getMyConsents().guardianMarketing`:
the full `marketing_line` text above, then either the unticked checkbox (not yet yes; saving calls
`updateMyMarketingConsent({ granted: true, consentTextId })`) or the withdraw button (yes; calls
`updateMyMarketingConsent({ granted: false, consentTextId })`, one press). The card says plainly what child information is used:
only the child's nickname, on the studio's internal follow-up list (`marketingLine.childNote`, round two, decision 4). Child rows and their withdraw buttons are unchanged. Errors use the same
`aria-live` region as the child rows.

## 4. Admin area

### 4.1 Route tree

```
/admin                         src/routes/admin/route.tsx           ssr:false · noindex · AdminLanguageProvider
├─ /admin/login                admin/login.tsx                      email + password (Lovable Cloud auth)
└─ (_staff guard, pathless)    admin/_staff/route.tsx               session check → getAdminMe → AdminShell
   ├─ /admin                   _staff/index.tsx                     dashboard                        P0 (cards P1+)
   ├─ /admin/inbox             _staff/inbox/index.tsx               booking requests                 P1
   │  └─ /admin/inbox/$requestId  _staff/inbox/$requestId.tsx       request detail                   P1 (+convert P3)
   ├─ /admin/sessions          _staff/sessions/index.tsx            weekend sessions                 P2
   │  └─ /admin/sessions/$sessionId _staff/sessions/$sessionId.tsx  roster, check-in/out, allergy chips  P3/P4
   ├─ /admin/families          _staff/families/index.tsx            search families · follow-up view (`?view=follow-up`)  P3 / P3-19
   │  └─ /admin/families/$familyId _staff/families/$familyId.tsx    tabs: overview · packages & payments ·
   │                                                                class history · consents · parent links  P3/P4/P5
   │  └─ /admin/families/$familyId/print  _staff/families/$familyId_.print.tsx  print sheet for signing (ssr: false, noindex)  P4-25
   ├─ /admin/payments          _staff/payments.tsx                  to check · paid this month       P3
   ├─ /admin/media             _staff/media.tsx                     clips × consent matrix           P4
   ├─ /admin/reports           _staff/reports.tsx                   aggregates                       P6
   ├─ /admin/privacy           _staff/privacy.tsx       (owner)     rights requests · incidents · audit log  P5
   └─ /admin/settings          _staff/settings.tsx      (owner)     staff roles · studio settings · consent texts  P1/P5
```

The brief also asked for "children, enrolments, consents" screens. To keep the admin small for 1–2 staff, they
live inside existing screens instead of separate routes:

| Area | Where |
|---|---|
| Children | Family page → Overview (cards per child: nickname, age from birth month, active, consent status chips, "ดูข้อมูลสุขภาพ") |
| Enrolments | Family page → Parent links (create/copy/revoke the enrol link; shows used/expired); "enrolment submitted" alerts link here |
| Consents | Family page → Consents tab (matrix per child × purpose, history, record paper/in-person/child consent); roster shows photo badges; `/admin/media` shows publishability |
| Health | Roster allergy chips + "ดูข้อมูลสุขภาพ" (audited) on roster and family page |

Route ids for `createFileRoute` include `_staff` (e.g. `"/admin/_staff/inbox/"`); URLs don't. Run `npx vite build`
after adding a route file so `routeTree.gen.ts` knows it before `tsc`. Typed `<Link to>` only accepts routes that
exist: **add each nav item in `AdminShell` in the same task that adds its route.**

### 4.2 Shell, guard, login, language ✅

- `src/routes/admin/route.tsx` sets `ssr: false` for the whole admin (the Lovable auth session is in `localStorage`)
  and `robots: noindex, nofollow`. Children inherit `ssr: false`.
- `_staff/route.tsx` `beforeLoad` redirects to `/admin/login?redirect=…` without a session; the component loads
  `getAdminMe`: no role → "ไม่มีสิทธิ์เข้าถึง / No access"; `Unauthorized` → login.
- Sign-out: `supabase.auth.signOut()`, `queryClient.clear()`, go to `/admin/login`.
- Any admin query/mutation error: `toAdminErrorCode(e)`; `unauthorized` → login with `redirect`; else toast
  `t.errors[code]`.
- Thai by default; the language toggle lives in the "More" menu; stored in `localStorage` `cherrylee-admin-lang`.

✅ `src/routes/admin/route.tsx`:

```tsx
import { createFileRoute, Outlet } from "@tanstack/react-router";
import { AdminLanguageProvider } from "@/i18n/AdminLanguageProvider";

/** Everything under /admin renders in the browser only (session is in localStorage, D5). */
export const Route = createFileRoute("/admin")({
  ssr: false,
  head: () => ({
    meta: [{ name: "robots", content: "noindex, nofollow" }, { title: "CherryLee & Co. · ทีมงาน" }],
  }),
  component: AdminRoot,
});

function AdminRoot() {
  return (
    <AdminLanguageProvider>
      <Outlet />
    </AdminLanguageProvider>
  );
}
```

✅ `src/routes/admin/login.tsx`:

```tsx
import { createFileRoute, useNavigate } from "@tanstack/react-router";
import { useState, type FormEvent } from "react";
import { Input } from "@/components/ui/input";
import { Label } from "@/components/ui/label";
import { supabase } from "@/integrations/supabase/client";
import { useAdminLanguage } from "@/i18n/AdminLanguageProvider";

type LoginSearch = { redirect?: string | undefined };

export const Route = createFileRoute("/admin/login")({
  validateSearch: (search: Record<string, unknown>): LoginSearch => {
    const r = search["redirect"];
    // Same-site admin paths only: no open redirects
    return {
      redirect:
        typeof r === "string" && r.startsWith("/admin") && !r.startsWith("//") ? r : undefined,
    };
  },
  component: LoginPage,
});

function LoginPage() {
  const { t } = useAdminLanguage();
  const { redirect } = Route.useSearch();
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [busy, setBusy] = useState(false);

  async function onSubmit(e: FormEvent<HTMLFormElement>) {
    e.preventDefault();
    const form = new FormData(e.currentTarget);
    setBusy(true);
    setError(null);
    const { error: signInError } = await supabase.auth.signInWithPassword({
      email: String(form.get("email") ?? "").trim(),
      password: String(form.get("password") ?? ""),
    });
    setBusy(false);
    if (signInError) {
      setError(t.login.failed); // one message for every failure: don't reveal which part was wrong
      return;
    }
    await navigate({ to: redirect ?? "/admin" });
  }

  return (
    <main className="mx-auto grid min-h-dvh max-w-sm content-center px-4 py-16">
      <h1 className="mb-1 text-h3">{t.login.title}</h1>
      <p className="mb-6 text-ink-soft">{t.login.lead}</p>
      <form onSubmit={onSubmit} noValidate className="grid gap-4">
        <div className="grid gap-1.5">
          <Label htmlFor="email">{t.login.email}</Label>
          <Input
            id="email"
            name="email"
            type="email"
            autoComplete="username"
            required
            className="min-h-11 bg-paper text-base"
          />
        </div>
        <div className="grid gap-1.5">
          <Label htmlFor="password">{t.login.password}</Label>
          <Input
            id="password"
            name="password"
            type="password"
            autoComplete="current-password"
            required
            className="min-h-11 bg-paper text-base"
          />
        </div>
        <p
          role="alert"
          aria-live="assertive"
          className="min-h-6 text-sm font-medium text-cherry-deep"
        >
          {error}
        </p>
        <button type="submit" className="btn btn-primary" disabled={busy}>
          {busy ? t.login.submitting : t.login.submit}
        </button>
        <p className="text-sm text-ink-soft">{t.login.help}</p>
      </form>
    </main>
  );
}
```

✅ `src/routes/admin/_staff/route.tsx`:

```tsx
import { createFileRoute, Outlet, redirect } from "@tanstack/react-router";
import { AdminShell } from "@/components/admin/AdminShell";
import { useAdminMe } from "@/components/admin/useAdminMe";
import { supabase } from "@/integrations/supabase/client";
import { useAdminLanguage } from "@/i18n/AdminLanguageProvider";
import { toAdminErrorCode } from "@/lib/backend/result";

/** Pathless guard for every signed-in admin page. UX only: each server function checks the role again. */
export const Route = createFileRoute("/admin/_staff")({
  beforeLoad: async ({ location }) => {
    const { data } = await supabase.auth.getSession();
    if (!data.session) {
      throw redirect({ to: "/admin/login", search: { redirect: location.href } });
    }
  },
  component: StaffLayout,
});

function StaffLayout() {
  const { t } = useAdminLanguage();
  const navigate = Route.useNavigate();
  const me = useAdminMe();

  if (me.isPending) return <p className="p-6">{t.common.loading}</p>;
  if (me.isError) {
    if (toAdminErrorCode(me.error) === "unauthorized") {
      void navigate({ to: "/admin/login" });
      return null;
    }
    return (
      <p role="alert" className="p-6">
        {t.errors.server_error}
      </p>
    );
  }
  if (!me.data.role) {
    return (
      <main className="mx-auto max-w-md p-6">
        <h1 className="mb-2 text-h3">{t.shell.noAccess.title}</h1>
        <p>{t.shell.noAccess.body}</p>
      </main>
    );
  }
  return (
    <AdminShell me={me.data}>
      <Outlet />
    </AdminShell>
  );
}
```

```ts
import { useQuery } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import { useServerFn } from "@tanstack/react-start";
import { getAdminMe } from "@/lib/backend/staff.functions";

/** The signed-in staff member (cached for the whole admin; cleared on sign-out). */
export function useAdminMe() {
  const fetchMe = useServerFn(getAdminMe);
  return useQuery({
    queryKey: ["admin", "me"],
    queryFn: () => fetchMe(),
    staleTime: 60_000,
    retry: false,
  });
}
```

import { useQueryClient } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import { Link, useNavigate } from "@tanstack/react-router";
import { CalendarDays, CreditCard, Inbox, LayoutGrid, Menu, Users } from "lucide-react";
import type { ReactNode } from "react";
import {
  DropdownMenu,
  DropdownMenuContent,
  DropdownMenuItem,
  DropdownMenuLabel,
  DropdownMenuSeparator,
  DropdownMenuTrigger,
} from "@/components/ui/dropdown-menu";
import { Toaster } from "@/components/ui/sonner";
import { supabase } from "@/integrations/supabase/client";
import { useAdminLanguage } from "@/i18n/AdminLanguageProvider";
import type { AdminMe } from "@/lib/backend/staff.schema";

/**
 * Admin frame: top bar + desktop side nav + mobile bottom tab bar (5 tabs, ≥ 44 px).
 * Add a nav item only when its route exists (each phase adds its own). Owner-only items are hidden
 * for staff, but the server still checks (this is UX only).
 */
const TABS = [
  { to: "/admin/inbox", key: "inbox", Icon: Inbox },
  { to: "/admin/sessions", key: "sessions", Icon: CalendarDays },
  { to: "/admin/families", key: "families", Icon: Users },
  { to: "/admin/payments", key: "payments", Icon: CreditCard },
] as const;
const MORE = [
  { to: "/admin/media", key: "media", ownerOnly: false },
  { to: "/admin/reports", key: "reports", ownerOnly: false },
  { to: "/admin/privacy", key: "privacy", ownerOnly: true },
  { to: "/admin/settings", key: "settings", ownerOnly: true },
] as const;

export function AdminShell({ me, children }: { me: AdminMe; children: ReactNode }) {
  const { t, lang, setLang } = useAdminLanguage();
  const queryClient = useQueryClient();
  const navigate = useNavigate();

  async function signOut() {
    await supabase.auth.signOut();
    queryClient.clear(); // drop every cached admin response from memory
    await navigate({ to: "/admin/login" });
  }

  const moreItems = MORE.filter((i) => !i.ownerOnly || me.role === "owner");

  return (
    <div className="min-h-dvh bg-cream pb-[calc(64px+env(safe-area-inset-bottom))] md:pb-0">
      <a
        href="#admin-main"
        className="sr-only focus:not-sr-only focus:absolute focus:left-2 focus:top-2"
      >
        {t.shell.skipLink}
      </a>
      <header className="sticky top-0 z-40 flex min-h-14 items-center gap-3 border-b-2 border-peach-deep bg-paper px-4">
        <Link
          to="/admin"
          className="mr-auto inline-flex min-h-11 items-center gap-2 font-display font-semibold no-underline"
        >
          <LayoutGrid className="h-5 w-5" aria-hidden="true" />
          CherryLee & Co.
        </Link>
        <DropdownMenu>
          <DropdownMenuTrigger className="inline-flex min-h-11 min-w-11 items-center justify-center gap-2 rounded-full border-2 border-ink px-3">
            <Menu className="h-5 w-5" aria-hidden="true" />
            <span className="sr-only md:not-sr-only">{t.shell.nav.more}</span>
          </DropdownMenuTrigger>
          <DropdownMenuContent align="end" className="min-w-56">
            <DropdownMenuLabel className="font-normal">
              {me.email ? t.shell.signedInAs(me.email) : null} ·{" "}
              {me.role ? t.shell.roles[me.role] : ""}
            </DropdownMenuLabel>
            <DropdownMenuSeparator />
            {moreItems.map((i) => (
              <DropdownMenuItem key={i.to} asChild className="min-h-11">
                <Link to={i.to}>{t.shell.nav[i.key]}</Link>
              </DropdownMenuItem>
            ))}
            <DropdownMenuSeparator />
            <DropdownMenuItem
              className="min-h-11"
              onSelect={() => setLang(lang === "th" ? "en" : "th")}
            >
              {t.shell.language}: {lang === "th" ? "English" : "ไทย"}
            </DropdownMenuItem>
            <DropdownMenuItem className="min-h-11" onSelect={() => void signOut()}>
              {t.shell.signOut}
            </DropdownMenuItem>
          </DropdownMenuContent>
        </DropdownMenu>
      </header>
      <div className="mx-auto flex max-w-6xl">
        <nav aria-label={t.shell.nav.more} className="hidden w-56 shrink-0 p-4 md:block">
          <ul role="list" className="grid gap-1">
            {TABS.map(({ to, key, Icon }) => (
              <li key={to}>
                <Link
                  to={to}
                  className="flex min-h-11 items-center gap-3 rounded-full px-4 text-ink no-underline hover:bg-cherry-soft"
                  activeProps={{
                    className: "bg-cherry-soft font-semibold",
                    "aria-current": "page",
                  }}
                >
                  <Icon className="h-5 w-5" aria-hidden="true" />
                  {t.shell.nav[key]}
                </Link>
              </li>
            ))}
          </ul>
        </nav>
        <main id="admin-main" className="min-w-0 flex-1 p-4 md:p-6">
          {children}
        </main>
      </div>
      {/* Mobile bottom tab bar */}
      <nav
        aria-label={t.shell.nav.more}
        className="fixed inset-x-0 bottom-0 z-40 border-t-2 border-peach-deep bg-paper pb-[env(safe-area-inset-bottom)] md:hidden"
      >
        <ul role="list" className="grid grid-cols-4">
          {TABS.map(({ to, key, Icon }) => (
            <li key={to}>
              <Link
                to={to}
                className="flex min-h-16 flex-col items-center justify-center gap-0.5 text-xs text-ink no-underline"
                activeProps={{
                  className: "font-semibold text-cherry-deep",
                  "aria-current": "page",
                }}
              >
                <Icon className="h-6 w-6" aria-hidden="true" />
                {t.shell.nav[key]}
              </Link>
            </li>
          ))}
        </ul>
      </nav>
      <Toaster position="top-center" />

}

✅ `src/i18n/AdminLanguageProvider.tsx`:

```tsx
import {
  createContext,
  useCallback,
  useContext,
  useEffect,
  useMemo,
  useState,
  type ReactNode,
} from "react";
import { adminContent, type AdminContent } from "@/content/admin";
import type { Lang } from "@/content/site";

const STORAGE_KEY = "cherrylee-admin-lang";

type AdminLanguageValue = { lang: Lang; setLang: (lang: Lang) => void; t: AdminContent };
const AdminLanguageContext = createContext<AdminLanguageValue | null>(null);

/** Thai by default; the choice is remembered on this device only (D23). */
export function AdminLanguageProvider({ children }: { children: ReactNode }) {
  const [lang, setLangState] = useState<Lang>("th");
  useEffect(() => {
    try {
      const v = window.localStorage.getItem(STORAGE_KEY);
      if (v === "th" || v === "en") setLangState(v);
    } catch {
      /* storage blocked: stay Thai */
    }
  }, []);
  const setLang = useCallback((next: Lang) => {
    setLangState(next);
    try {
      window.localStorage.setItem(STORAGE_KEY, next);
    } catch {
      /* ignore */
    }
  }, []);
  useEffect(() => {
    document.documentElement.lang = lang;
  }, [lang]);
  const value = useMemo(() => ({ lang, setLang, t: adminContent[lang] }), [lang, setLang]);
  return <AdminLanguageContext.Provider value={value}>{children}</AdminLanguageContext.Provider>;
}

export function useAdminLanguage(): AdminLanguageValue {
  const ctx = useContext(AdminLanguageContext);
  if (!ctx) throw new Error("useAdminLanguage must be used inside <AdminLanguageProvider>");
  return ctx;
}
```

Mobile bottom bar: the architect listed five tabs (กล่องข้อความ, รอบเรียน, ครอบครัว, ชำระเงิน, เพิ่มเติม). The
"เพิ่มเติม / More" tab is the `DropdownMenu` in the top bar (media, reports, privacy, settings, language, sign out),
so the bottom bar shows the first four; this keeps every target ≥ 64 px wide on a 360 px phone.

### 4.3 Screen specs

Dates: `formatSessionDate(…, lang)` (Buddhist year in Thai); timestamps as "เสาร์ 18 ต.ค. 2569 · 10:42"
(write a small `formatDateTime(iso, lang)` next to `formatSessionDate` when first needed, in Asia/Bangkok).
Money: `formatBaht` from `business.ts`. Every list has loading (`Skeleton` rows), empty and error states.
Every write: button busy state → toast `t.common.saved` → invalidate the query keys in `20-server-api.md` § 11.

#### `/admin` dashboard (P0 shell, cards from P1)

Cards (each a link, `min-h-16`), built only from existing functions:
`inbox.newCount` (`listBookingRequests({ filter: "new" })`) · failed alerts (rows with `alertStatus = failed`) ·
next open session with seats (`listSessions` for the next 14 days, P2) · payments to check (`listPayments("pending")`,
P3) · media needing takedown (`listMediaAssets`, P4) · consent alerts still `pending`/`failed` (P4, § 5.1
`dashboard.consentAlerts(n)`) · files waiting for deletion (owner, `listStorageCleanup`, P5 core) · rights requests due
within 7 days (owner, `listDsrRequests`, **parked**). Nothing waiting → `dashboard.allClear`. Each card is added by
the card that adds its data source.

**Alerts not sent** (card P4-19, D29, R-30): under the cards, a short list from `listPendingStaffAlerts` (inbox
functions, 20 § 9.3): one row per `enrolment_submitted` / `consent_withdrawn` notification that is `pending` or
`failed`: `labels.notificationKind[kind]` · `labels.notifyStatus[status]` · time · **"เปิดหน้าครอบครัว / Open family"**
(`dashboard.openFamily`, link to `/admin/families/$familyId` when `familyId` is known) · **"ส่งอีกครั้ง / Resend"**
(`dashboard.resend`) → `retryStaffNotification`. The count feeds `dashboard.consentAlerts(n)`. Booking-request alerts
stay on the request page (they already have a resend button there).

#### `/admin/inbox` (P1) ✅ skeleton

Search params `?filter=&page=&q=`. Tabs = filters (`active` default). Search box (debounced 300 ms, ≤ 50 chars) updates
`?q=`. Columns: code (link) · parent name · children count (`sm+`) · preferred date (`md+`) · status badge + "แจ้งเตือนไม่สำเร็จ"
badge when the alert failed. `status = new` rows are bold, and the page title shows "ใหม่ N". Pagination (30/page)
with Previous/Next buttons and `common.page(n, of)`. Empty: `inbox.empty.active | other | search`.

```tsx
import { keepPreviousData, useQuery } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import { createFileRoute, Link } from "@tanstack/react-router";
import { useServerFn } from "@tanstack/react-start";
import { Badge } from "@/components/ui/badge";
import {
  Table,
  TableBody,
  TableCell,
  TableHead,
  TableHeader,
  TableRow,
} from "@/components/ui/table";
import { Tabs, TabsList, TabsTrigger } from "@/components/ui/tabs";
import { useAdminLanguage } from "@/i18n/AdminLanguageProvider";
import { formatSessionDate } from "@/lib/backend/dates";
import { listBookingRequests } from "@/lib/backend/inbox.functions";
import { InboxFilterSchema, type InboxFilter } from "@/lib/backend/inbox.schema";

type InboxSearch = {
  filter?: InboxFilter | undefined;
  page?: number | undefined;
  q?: string | undefined;
};
const FILTERS: InboxFilter[] = [
  "active",
  "new",
  "contacted",
  "waitlisted",
  "converted",
  "closed",
  "spam",
  "all",
];

export const Route = createFileRoute("/admin/_staff/inbox/")({
  validateSearch: (search: Record<string, unknown>): InboxSearch => {
    const f = InboxFilterSchema.safeParse(search["filter"]);
    const p = Number(search["page"]);
    const q = search["q"];
    return {
      filter: f.success ? f.data : undefined,
      page: Number.isInteger(p) && p > 0 ? p : undefined,
      q: typeof q === "string" && q.length <= 50 ? q : undefined,
    };
  },
  component: InboxPage,
});

function InboxPage() {
  const { t, lang } = useAdminLanguage();
  const { filter = "active", page = 0, q } = Route.useSearch();
  const navigate = Route.useNavigate();
  const list = useServerFn(listBookingRequests);
  const query = useQuery({
    queryKey: ["admin", "inbox", filter, page, q ?? ""],
    queryFn: () => list({ data: { filter, page, ...(q ? { q } : {}) } }),
    placeholderData: keepPreviousData,
  });

  return (
    <>
      <h1 className="mb-4 text-h3">
        {t.inbox.title}
        {query.data && query.data.newCount > 0 ? (
          <Badge className="ml-2 align-middle">{t.inbox.newBadge(query.data.newCount)}</Badge>
        ) : null}
      </h1>
      <Tabs
        value={filter}
        onValueChange={(v) => void navigate({ search: { filter: InboxFilterSchema.parse(v) } })}
      >
        <TabsList className="mb-4 flex h-auto flex-wrap justify-start">
          {FILTERS.map((f) => (
            <TabsTrigger key={f} value={f} className="min-h-11">
              {t.inbox.filters[f]}
            </TabsTrigger>
          ))}
        </TabsList>
      </Tabs>
      {query.isPending ? (
        <p>{t.common.loading}</p>
      ) : query.isError ? (
        <p role="alert">{t.common.loadError}</p>
      ) : query.data.rows.length === 0 ? (
        <p>
          {q
            ? t.inbox.empty.search
            : filter === "active"
              ? t.inbox.empty.active
              : t.inbox.empty.other}
        </p>
      ) : (
        <Table>
          <TableHeader>
            <TableRow>
              <TableHead>{t.inbox.columns.ref}</TableHead>
              <TableHead>{t.inbox.columns.name}</TableHead>
              <TableHead className="hidden sm:table-cell">{t.inbox.columns.children}</TableHead>
              <TableHead className="hidden md:table-cell">{t.inbox.columns.when}</TableHead>
              <TableHead>{t.inbox.columns.status}</TableHead>
            </TableRow>
          </TableHeader>
          <TableBody>
            {query.data.rows.map((r) => (
              <TableRow key={r.id} className={r.status === "new" ? "font-semibold" : undefined}>
                <TableCell>
                  <Link
                    to="/admin/inbox/$requestId"
                    params={{ requestId: r.id }}
                    className="inline-flex min-h-11 items-center"
                  >
                    {r.refCode}
                  </Link>
                </TableCell>
                <TableCell>{r.guardianName}</TableCell>
                <TableCell className="hidden sm:table-cell">
                  {t.inbox.childrenCount(r.childrenCount)}
                </TableCell>
                <TableCell className="hidden md:table-cell">
                  {r.preferredDate
                    ? formatSessionDate(r.preferredDate, lang, { short: true })
                    : t.common.none}
                </TableCell>
                <TableCell className="flex flex-wrap gap-1">
                  <Badge variant="secondary">{t.labels.requestStatus[r.status]}</Badge>
                  {r.alertStatus === "failed" ? (
                    <Badge variant="destructive">{t.inbox.alertFailed}</Badge>
                  ) : null}
                </TableCell>
              </TableRow>
            ))}
          </TableBody>
        </Table>
      )}
    </>
  );
}
```

#### `/admin/inbox/$requestId` (P1; convert P3)

Data `getBookingRequest`. Layout (cards, one column on phones):
- Header: `request.title(ref)`, status badge, `received(when)`.
- **Contact**: name; preferred channel (`labels.contactPreference`); phone with **"โทร"** (`tel:` link, ≥ 44 px) when
  given. LINE (D26): show `request.lineHowTo` ("รอผู้ปกครองส่งรหัส {ref} มาที่ @cherryleeandco แล้วตอบในแชตนั้น /
  Wait for the parent to send {ref} to @cherryleeandco, then reply in that chat") and, when a LINE ID was given, it as a
  **text hint only** (no copy button, rev 14 Oct 2026, decision 4). Staff find the chat by the ref code, never by the LINE ID. Never offer "add friend by ID".
- **Children**: `request.child(n, nickname, age)` per item.
- **Interested in**: package (`labels.packageCode`, `none` when null), day, chosen session (P2), form language,
  privacy-notice version.
- **Message** (or `noMessage`).
- **Team**: status buttons (`markContacted`, `markWaitlisted`, `markClosed`, `markSpam` with an `AlertDialog`,
  `reopen` from closed/spam → `new`), staff note `Textarea` (≤ 1000, hint `staffNoteHint`, Save) → `updateBookingRequest`.
  Shows `handledBy` (email via the staff list if available, else "–") and `contactedAt`.
- **LINE alerts**: list of attempts (`labels.notifyStatus`, attempts, time); when the latest is `failed`, show
  `alerts.failedHelp` and **"ส่งแจ้งเตือนอีกครั้ง"** → `retryStaffNotification`. Never show `last_error` raw to staff
  beyond its short code.
- P3: **"สร้างครอบครัวจากคำขอนี้"** opens `convertDialog` prefilled (family name = guardian name, guardian phone and
  LINE ID from the request (editable), children nicknames from the request; nickname required here) →
  `createFamilyFromRequest` → navigate to the family page. A LINE-path request may have neither a phone nor a LINE ID
  (D26), and the OA chat does not show the parent's LINE ID: then the dialog shows `convertDialog.contactNeeded` above
  the two fields and the submit stays disabled until one is filled (zod `contact_required`, R-38). `convertDialog.contactNeeded` (rev 14 Oct 2026) asks for a phone number, or says the parent can message @cherryleeandco first. It never asks staff to copy a LINE ID. Open question 2 (`20-server-api.md` § 9.5a) decides whether a LINE-path family may skip this step. The dialog also shows `MarketingConsentBlock` (§ 2.6, mode `ask`) below the contact fields, unticked, with the full text above it, when `getGuardianMarketing` returns a text. Otherwise the box is hidden and `marketingLine` is sent as `null`. When converted,
  show `convertedTo` link instead.

#### `/admin/sessions` (P2)

Search `?month=YYYY-MM` (default: current Bangkok month). Header with previous/next month buttons and
`sessions.monthLabel`. Data: `listSessions` (one `get_sessions_with_seats` call). Table: date (link → roster from P3) ·
status badge · capacity (`noCapacity`) · held offline · booked (P3) · seats left · public note (truncated). Row action
"แก้ไข" opens a `Sheet` with `SaveSessionInput` fields (`edit.*` labels; date picker limited to weekends via
`isWeekendDate`, error `edit.notWeekend`). From P3 on, choosing status **cancelled** on a session with `booked > 0`
opens an `AlertDialog` (`sessions.cancelWarning(n)`, § 5.1) before saving: the database then cancels those bookings
and returns their credits (R-23).
"สร้างรอบเสาร์–อาทิตย์" opens a `Dialog`: from (default: next Saturday), weeks (default 8, 1–12), capacity
(default `studio_settings.default_session_capacity`, may be empty) → `generateWeekendSessions` → toast `done(n)`.
Empty: `sessions.empty`.

#### `/admin/sessions/$sessionId` roster (P3; health & photo badges P4)

Header: `roster.title(date)`, status, `summary(booked, seatsLeft)`. Table/cards (cards on phones):
child nickname · family (link) · booking status · **allergy cell** (P4, from `getSessionHealthFlags`; state from
`healthRosterState(flag)`, R-07 — never show "no allergies" unless a parent reported none):
`allergies` → one `Badge` per allergen label, "+ อื่นๆ / + other" when `hasOtherAllergens`, a "มีหมายเหตุ / notes"
marker when `hasFoodsToAvoid` or `hasCareNotes`; `reported_none` → `health.reportedNone(date of healthUpdatedAt)`;
`no_info` → `health.noInfo` ("ยังไม่มีข้อมูลการแพ้อาหาร ถามครอบครัวก่อนทำอาหารนะคะ / No allergy info — check with the
family before cooking") as a **warning badge with text**, plus `health.removedOn(date)` when `allergyCheckNeededAt`
is set. When G-HEALTH = paper (`HEALTH_IN_SYSTEM = false` in `src/lib/backend/public-config.ts`, R-29), every child
shows `health.paperForm` instead and the health buttons are hidden · photo badge
(`photoBadge[photoStatus]`, from one `consent_matrix` call) · in/out times · actions:
- **เช็กอิน** (when confirmed/held) → `updateBookingStatus({ action: "check_in" })`.
- **เช็กเอาต์** → `pickupDialog`: radio list of the family's guardians and **active** authorised pickup contacts
  (name + relationship + phone), required; `none` text when the family has no pickup contacts → `check_out`.
- **ขาด** (round two, option ก) → `AlertDialog absentConfirm` → `updateBookingStatus({ action: "mark_absent" })`. The absence **uses one credit**, as booked. Shown on `held` or `confirmed` rows only.
- **ยกเลิกการจอง** → `cancelConfirm`, which shows the `notice` fields (`sessions.notice`): channel radio **ทาง LINE / On LINE** or **ที่สตูดิโอ / In person at the studio**, then the time (`datetime-local`, required, not in the future; hint `lineHint` or `inPersonHint`). Submit → `cancelBooking({ id, noticeChannel, noticeAt })` (`20-server-api.md` § 9.7). The result toast is `notice.cancelDone[status]`: `cancelled` keeps the credit; `cancelled_late` uses one. Shown on `held` or `confirmed` rows only. The roster shows `cancelled_late` as a grey row with the badge from `bookingStatus.cancelled_late`, and the row keeps its seat.
- **จองชดเชย / Book a make-up** (rev 14 Oct 2026, round two): shown on `held` or `confirmed` rows (step a runs first) and on `cancelled` rows (step a is skipped). Not shown on `absent`, `attended` or `cancelled_late`. Opens `MakeUpDialog` (`src/components/admin/sessions/MakeUpDialog.tsx`). **Step a (only for `held` or `confirmed`):** the same `notice` fields, then `cancelBooking`. `cancelled_late` stops the dialog with `makeUp.noticeShort(n)`. **Step b:** pick an open session, then one of the family's packages (`package_balances`, `left(n)`), then `createBooking` with `makeUpOfBookingId` set (`20-server-api.md` § 9.7). The lead `makeUp.lead(n)` uses `settings.cancelNoticeHours` from `getStudioSettings`. Errors: `make_up_notice_short` → `makeUp.noticeShort(n)`; `invalid_state` → `makeUp.notEligible`; `conflict` → `makeUp.alreadyMade`; `no_credit_left`, `session_full`, `already_booked` → existing `errors` copy. The make-up step never asks for a second notice. No public copy: the rule stays off the website (decision 10).
- **ดูข้อมูลสุขภาพ** (per child with a flag) → button text + small note `health.logged`; opens a `Dialog`,
  calls `getChildHealth({ data: { childId, includeHealth: true } })` **only on press** (the staff full-notes dialog passes the flag as true), shows allergens/other/avoid/care; data is dropped when the dialog
  closes. "แก้ไขข้อมูลการแพ้อาหาร" inside it → the same four fields as `HealthSection` (copy its field markup, including the grouped allergen checklist with `ALLERGEN_SCREEN_GROUPS` (round two: ten keys, peanut and tree_nut under one "ถั่ว" group); labels from `adminContent.health` and `labels.allergens`) → `saveChildHealth`; `consent_required`
  error shows `health.noConsent`.
"เพิ่มเด็กในรอบนี้" → `addBooking` dialog: search family (reuse `listFamilies`), child select, package select showing
`left(n)` from `package_balances` (or `noPackage`), status held/confirmed → `createBooking`; errors
`session_full`, `no_credit_left`, `already_booked` map to `t.errors`.

#### `/admin/families` (P3)

Search box (`?q=`, debounced), status filter tabs, "เพิ่มครอบครัว" (`Dialog` → `saveFamily` without id, then
navigate to the new family). List as cards on phones / table on `md+`: family name · children
("น้องมิว 8 ปี, น้องเมฆ") · main parent · sessions left (sum of active packages) · last activity. Empty:
`families.empty` / `emptySearch`.

##### `/admin/families?view=follow-up` (P3-19; rev 14 Oct 2026, owner decision 2)

A second view of the same route, chosen by a tab `families.followUp.tab` beside the status tabs (`?view=follow-up`; the
default is `view=all`). **Read only.**

- Data: `listFollowUpFamilies({ page })` (`20-server-api.md` § 9.5a), 30 per page. **One row per granted guardian** (owner decision 5, round two): a family with two guardians who said yes appears on two rows, with the same family label.
- Columns on `md+`, cards on phones: family label · guardian name (`guardianName`) · yes since (`consentedAt`, `formatDateTime`). The label is `familyLabelTh` / `familyLabelEn` from the view, built by the database (decision 4, round two): **ครอบครัวน้อง + ชื่อเล่น** in Thai (for example "ครอบครัวน้องมะลิ"), and **Family of [nickname]** in English. Show both as they come; do not rebuild them in the screen. A family with no active child is not listed. Two granted guardians of one family give two rows with the same label (decision 5). Nickname only: no full name, age or health data appears on the page. No preferred contact and no last-class column.
- Label copy function: `families.followUp.familyLabel(nickname)` returns the Thai or English string by the admin language. The nickname is rendered as plain text, never in a URL, a `title` attribute or an export.
- The only action per row is a link "เปิดครอบครัว / Open family" to `/admin/families/$familyId`. **No checkboxes, no bulk action,
  no export, no send button.** The lead `families.followUp.lead` says that contact is by hand from the studio's own LINE chat.
- `families.followUp.noNames` sits under the table: this page shows only a child's nickname (inside the label), never a full name, age, health data or LINE ID. It is staff only and is never shown publicly.
- Empty: `families.followUp.empty`.

#### `/admin/families/$familyId` (P3 → P5)

`Tabs` (search `?tab=`):
- **Banners above the tabs** (§ 5.1 copy): `family.restrictedBanner(date, note)` when `processingRestrictedAt` is set
  (D28; red-bordered, text not colour only; new bookings, links, granted consents and profile edits are refused);
  `family.scheduledDeletion(date)` when `retainUntil` is set (inactive 12 months); per guardian with
  `pendingLinkChanges`: `family.linkChangesBanner` listing the changed fields with their **old** values and a button
  `family.confirmLinkChanges` → `saveGuardian({ …, confirmLinkChanges: true })` (R-21).
- **ภาพรวม / Overview**: guardians (cards: name, phone `tel:`, LINE ID as plain text with no copy button (rev 14 Oct 2026), preferred contact badge, marketing chip (`marketingLine.statusGranted` / `statusNotGranted`), email, primary badge,
  `identityChecked(how, when)` or `identityNotChecked`, `responsibility` tick; edit `Sheet` with `guardianForm`, including the `responsibility` checkbox for a statement given on paper or in
person; a `responsibility_required` error from consent recording points here. The `isPrimary` checkbox is shown
ticked and **disabled** on the current primary guardian (a family always keeps one; make another guardian primary
instead, R-39); on any other guardian it starts unticked);
  children (cards: nickname, age from `birth_month`, else `reportedAge(reported_age_years)`, else `ageUnknown`; active; consent status chips per purpose (P4); "ดูข้อมูลสุขภาพ"
  (P4); edit `Sheet` with `childForm`); contacts (list with role badges; add/edit `Sheet` with `contactForm`
  including the required informed checkbox; remove with `deleteContactConfirm` → `deleteFamilyContact`, a soft
delete that keeps pickup history, R-22); staff note (`staffNoteHint`). Children cards show `family.allergyRemoved(date)`
when `allergyCheckNeededAt` is set (P4).
- **Owner card "ความเป็นส่วนตัว (เจ้าของ) / Privacy (owner)"** (Stage B, P5-03; only when `me.role === "owner"`):
  **ส่งออกข้อมูล** (`exportFamilyData` → download `family-export-YYYY-MM-DD.json` via a Blob; show `privacy.exportNote`),
  **ระงับการใช้ข้อมูล / ยกเลิกการระงับ** (`setFamilyRestriction` with a short note field `privacy.restrictNoteLabel`,
  `AlertDialog` `privacy.restrictConfirm`), **ลบข้อมูลครอบครัว** (`AlertDialog` with `eraseBody` and a text input
  labelled `eraseConfirmLabel(familyName)`; button disabled until it matches exactly) → `eraseFamily` → navigate to
  `/admin/families` and show `privacy.erasedThenFiles` (run "files to delete" today).
- **แพ็กเกจและการชำระเงิน**: packages with `balance(left, total)`, purchase date, status (packages never expire, decision 10, D36); "เพิ่มแพ็กเกจ"
  (`packages.*`; price shown read-only from `PRICES`); per package "ปิดแพ็กเกจ / Close" (staff) and, owner only,
  "ยกเลิก (void) / Void" → `updatePackagePurchase` (R-23); payments for this family with the
  same row actions as `/admin/payments`; "บันทึกการชำระเงิน".
- **ประวัติการเรียน**: last 50 bookings: date · child · status · in/out · picked up by.
- **ความยินยอม** (P4): per child a matrix (rows = 5 purposes; columns `guardianCol`, `childCol` (only when
  `needsChildConsent`), `statusCol`), history list (`labels.consentDecision/Channel/Method`, text version, recorded
  by, evidence link → `getConsentEvidenceUrl`); "บันทึกความยินยอม" `Dialog` (`consents.form.*`; optional evidence
  upload first via `uploadConsentEvidence`; for `givenBy = child` the channel is fixed to "ที่สตูดิโอ" and the
  child script (§ 4.4) is shown in a `Collapsible` above the form).
- **ลิงก์สำหรับผู้ปกครอง** (P4): enrol link card (`links.enrolTitle/Lead`; create → a `Dialog` showing the URL
  once with **"คัดลอกลิงก์"** and `createdLead`; list of links with `labels.linkState` + `expires(date)` + revoke
  with confirm) and the manage link card (`createManage`, 90 days). Create buttons are disabled with
  `family.restrictedShort` while the family is restricted.

##### Family page changes (rev 14 Oct 2026)

- **Guardian sheet** (P3-11, `guardianForm`): one new field, `preferredContact` (a `RadioGroup`: LINE / phone; none selected =
  not recorded). `phone` needs a phone number (`phone_required`). **Save** calls `saveGuardian` first. Then, only if the marketing
  box is ticked and the guardian is not yet yes, it calls `recordGuardianMarketing({ granted: true, consentTextId, channel })`,
  with the channel radio. The box (`MarketingConsentBlock`, mode `manage`) never withdraws on Save. Its **withdraw** button calls
  `recordGuardianMarketing({ granted: false, consentTextId, channel })` directly. A failed marketing step after a saved sheet
  keeps the sheet open and shows `errors[code]` in an `aria-live` region.
- **Packages:** no expiry field and no expiry text (decision 10, D36). Void stays owner-only.
- **Consents tab:** one button `families.print.button` ("พิมพ์ใบลงทะเบียน / Print enrolment record") → `/admin/families/$familyId/print` (card P4-25, step 6).
- Banners, guardians' `pendingLinkChanges` and the owner privacy card are unchanged.

#### `/admin/families/$familyId/print` (P4-25; owner decision 5, 14 Oct 2026)

Route file `src/routes/admin/_staff/families/$familyId_.print.tsx`. The trailing `_` keeps it outside the family tabs layout
(confirm the built path after `npx vite build`). `ssr: false`, `noindex`, inside `AdminShell`. `@media print` hides the shell,
the navigation, the toolbar and every button (`print:hidden`).

1. **Toolbar (hidden in print):** one unticked checkbox `families.print.includeHealth`, and one button `families.print.printNow`
   ("พิมพ์หรือบันทึกเป็น PDF / Print or save as PDF"). The checkbox copy says that the allergen checklist always prints, that foods to avoid and care notes print only when it is ticked, and
   that every print is recorded.
2. **One press does three things, in order:** `getPrintSheet({ data: { familyId, includeHealth } })` (POST; audited inside the
   server function, `20-server-api.md` § 9.16) → render the sheet → `window.print()` once the sheet has rendered. If the call fails,
   nothing is rendered or printed, and `errors[code]` shows in an `aria-live` region. Nothing is fetched on page load.
3. **Sheet, in this order:** header (studio name, family display name, `families.print.printedAt(date)`); guardians (full name,
   phone, primary badge, parental responsibility, marketing as yes or no); children (nickname, birth month or reported age,
   active); active authorised and emergency contacts (name, relationship, phone, roles); the **consent record**: per child, one row
   per purpose returned, with the status text, given by, face level, decided date and the text title, version and hash prefix
   (`families.print.noAnswer` when there is none; the consent text body is not printed). The **health block** (`families.print.healthHeading`) prints for every child with a health record: the allergen checklist and the other-allergies text, always. The foods to avoid and care notes print only when `includeHealth` is true; otherwise `families.print.healthOff` stands in their place and no value of those two appears on the sheet.
   Round two (decisions 3 and 7, 14 Oct 2026), inside the health block, per child:
   - **Allergen checklist, large and readable for class use.** One row for each key in `ALLERGENS` (ten rows), in the order of
     `ALLERGEN_SCREEN_GROUPS`. The nut keys are one group headed `allergens.nutsGroup` ("ถั่ว" / "Nuts") with two rows. Each row
     is a box (☑ when the key is in `health.allergens`, ☐ when not), then the TH label and the EN label from § 5.2 side by side. Text
     at least 16 pt in print, each row at least 9 mm tall, the box at least 6 mm. Ticked rows are bold and say "แพ้ / Allergic" in text,
     so the meaning does not depend on colour.
   - A note under the checklist: `families.print.notTicked` ("ไม่ได้ติ๊ก = ไม่ได้แจ้งไว้ในแบบฟอร์ม / Not ticked = not reported on the form").
     When the child has no health record (`health: null`), the checklist is replaced by `families.print.noHealthRecord` ("ยังไม่มีข้อมูลการแพ้อาหาร ถามครอบครัวก่อนทำอาหารนะคะ / No allergy information yet. Check with the family before cooking.").
     Never print "no allergies" unless the parent reported none (R-07).
   - Then `families.print.otherAllergens`, `families.print.foodsToAvoid` and `families.print.careNotes` (the free text, as saved).
   The checklist is read-only: no checkbox is interactive on the print route.
4. **Two signature blocks:** guardian signature and date; staff signature and date (`families.print.sigGuardian`,
   `families.print.sigStaff`, `families.print.sigDate`). Each signature line is at least 12 mm tall.
5. **Footer:** `families.print.noStore` and the prepared date.
- `processingRestricted` shows `families.print.restricted` above the sheet. The restriction note is never printed.
- The sheet lives in component state only. It is cleared when the route unmounts. It is never written to localStorage,
  sessionStorage or the query cache (`gcTime: 0`). Changing the health tick clears the rendered sheet, so the paper always
  matches the tick.
- **Entry:** one button in the family's **Consents tab** (card P4-25, step 6), at least 44 px.
- Print styles: black text on white, no colour-only status, a real heading for the health block.

#### `/admin/payments` (P3)

status · receipt no. · slip ("ดูสลิป" → `getSlipUrl` → open in a new tab; never stored) · actions: **ยืนยันว่าชำระแล้ว**
(`updatePayment({ markPaid: true })`), **แนบสลิป** (file input `accept="image/jpeg,image/png,image/webp,application/pdf"`,
check size ≤ 5 MB in the browser too, `FormData` → `uploadPaymentSlip`), owner only: void/refund (`AlertDialog`
`voidTitle/voidBody`) and **ลบสลิป / Delete slip** (`AlertDialog` `payments.deleteSlipConfirm`, § 5.1) →
`deletePaymentSlip` (R-23). Record dialog fields `payments.form.*` (incl. optional `note`) and shows `noBankDetails` as a hint. Empty: `payments.empty[tab]`.

#### `/admin/media` (P4)

Lead text, "เพิ่มสื่อ" (`Sheet`, `media.form.*` incl. `showsFaces` with `showsFacesHint` and `onParentsGroup`,
`titleHint`). Table: media title (+ kind) · children in it (chips;
"เลือกเด็กในภาพ" opens a multi-select → `setMediaAssetChildren`) · five channel cells
(`parentsGroup`, `website`, `social`, `youtube`, `print`; print added 14 Oct 2026): **icon + text** from `ChannelState` (`20-server-api.md` § 14.7):
`ok` → ✓ `publishable`, `blocked` → ✗ `notPublishable`, `not_used` → – `notUsed`, `check_manually` → `checkManually`
(legacy-only clip on a channel its old confirmation didn't cover; never counted as a takedown, R-11) · status. Rows with `needsTakedown` get a
