(`markMediaTakenDown`). The six clips in `src/content/videos.ts` are already seeded by `0005` (`10-data-model.md` § 14); F4-07 links their
children here.

#### `/admin/reports` (P6)

Date range (from/to, default last 3 months). Sections: requests per week (simple bar chart with the shadcn `chart`
or a table; status breakdown) · `conversion(c, t)` · seats per session (table) · attendance per month (table:
attended/absent/rate) · revenue per month (owner only; staff see `revenueOwnerOnly`) · `credits(n)`. **CSV download**
built in the browser from the same data (one file per section, UTF-8 with BOM so Excel shows Thai), with
`noPersonalData` shown next to the button. Empty: `reports.empty`.

#### `/admin/privacy` (owner, P5)

**Stage B ships only the "ไฟล์รอลบ / Files to delete" tab (card P5-04)**; export, erase and restriction live on the
family page owner card. The DSR, breach and audit tabs below are **parked** (D27, cards PK-02) until the owner
un-parks them (G-PARK); meanwhile the owner follows `50-security-privacy.md` §§ 5, 18, 20 with a private spreadsheet.

Tabs (full design, for when they are un-parked):
- **คำขอใช้สิทธิ**: table (received · requester · right · channel · status · due). The "due" cell shows
  `reviewDue(days)` until identity is verified/reviewed, then `dueIn(days)` or `overdue(days)` (red badge + text).
  Form `Sheet` with `dsr.form.*` (incl. `requesterContact` and `details`; refusal reason required when refused). Family-linked requests show **ส่งออกข้อมูล**
  (`exportFamilyData` → download `family-export-YYYY-MM-DD.json` via a Blob; show `exportNote`) and **ลบข้อมูลครอบครัว**
  (`AlertDialog` with `eraseBody` and a text input labelled `eraseConfirmLabel(familyName)`; button disabled until it
  matches exactly) → `eraseFamily`.
- **เหตุข้อมูลรั่วไหล**: table with a live countdown `hoursLeft(h)` to `pdpc_deadline` (`deadlinePassed` after);
  form `breach.form.*` (incl. `description`, never the leaked data itself); when `riskLevel = high` show `highRiskNote`; a link button `pdpcLink` →
  https://www.pdpc.or.th/17259/ (new tab).
- **บันทึกการเข้าถึง**: `listAuditLog` table (time · actor email or `system` · action · table · row id), filter by
  table, paged. Never shows field values (the log has none).
- **ไฟล์รอลบ / Files to delete**: `files.lead`, `listStorageCleanup` table (storage · reason · queued · state:
  `pending` / `deleted` / `failed`), button **"ลบไฟล์ที่รออยู่"** → `runStorageCleanup` → toast `files.done(d, f)`.
  Empty: `files.empty`. Show a count badge on the tab when files are waiting (also a dashboard card for the owner).

#### `/admin/settings` (owner; consent texts F5-05)

- **บัญชีทีมงาน**: lead (create users in Lovable first; same-day offboarding), table (email · role · last sign-in);
  "ให้สิทธิ์ทีมงาน" email input → `setStaffRole` (`not_found` shows `addHint`); "ถอนสิทธิ์" with confirm (hidden on
  the owner's own row: `self`).
- **การตั้งค่าสตูดิโอ**: four controls with hints (`bookingFormOpen`, `staffAlertsEnabled` as `Switch`;
  `defaultCapacity` number input, may be empty; `cancelNoticeHours` number input, 0–72, default 3, hint `settings.cancelNoticeHint`) → `updateStudioSettings`. Staff (non-owner) never reach this page;
  `getStudioSettings` is used elsewhere for defaults.
- **ตรวจระบบ / System check** (card P1-19, F1-10, R-30): a button "ตรวจตอนนี้ / Check now" → `getSystemCheck`
  (owner). Shows one row per secret name (`IP_HASH_SECRET`, `TURNSTILE_SECRET_KEY`, `LINE_ALERT_CHANNEL_ID`,
  `LINE_ALERT_CHANNEL_SECRET`, `LINE_ALERT_TO`, `PUBLIC_SITE_URL`) with ✓ `settings.systemCheck.present` or ✗
  `settings.systemCheck.missing` (never the value), and one row "visitor IP seen" ✓/✗. Copy keys in § 5.1.
- **ข้อความขอความยินยอม** (F5-05, **parked**, card PK-03; `publishConsentText` calls the one-transaction SQL
  function): table of versions (purpose · version · language · in use from · retired ·
  checksum first 8 chars). "เผยแพร่ฉบับใหม่" `Dialog` with both languages side by side (`texts.form.*`; the title is
  the checkbox sentence) → `publishConsentText`. "เลิกใช้ฉบับนี้" with confirm → `retireConsentText`.

### 4.4 Child consent script (10+) 📝

Staff read this in person on the first day (PDPA s.20(1), D11), then record each answer with
`recordStaffConsent({ givenBy: "child", channel: "in_person", method: "staff_recorded_verbal" })`. It is part of
`adminContent.childScript` (§ 5) and shown on the family page's Consents tab. Principles: talk at the child's level,
one question per topic, "no" is always fine and is never questioned, ask only about channels the guardian already
allowed, never in front of other children, never on LINE.

| Step | TH | EN |
|---|---|---|
| Open | สวัสดี{ชื่อเล่น} วันนี้ครูขอถามอะไรหน่อยนะ ไม่มีคำตอบผิด จะตอบว่า "ไม่" ก็ได้นะ | Hi {nickname}! Can I ask you something? There are no wrong answers, and it’s fine to say no. |
| Change of mind | ถ้าเปลี่ยนใจทีหลัง บอกครูหรือบอกคุณพ่อคุณแม่ได้ตลอดเลย | If you change your mind later, you can tell me or your mum or dad any time. |
| Health | คุณพ่อคุณแม่บอกครูว่าหนูแพ้อาหารบางอย่าง ครูอยากจำไว้ จะได้ทำอาหารด้วยกันอย่างปลอดภัย หนูโอเคไหมที่ครูรู้เรื่องนี้ | Your mum or dad told me you’re allergic to some foods. I’d like to remember that so we can cook together safely. Is it okay with you that I know this? |
| Media | บางทีครูถ่ายรูปหรือวิดีโอตอนเราเล่นละครหรือทำอาหาร แล้วเอาไปลง{ช่องทาง} หนูอยากให้ครูใช้รูปของหนูไหม ถ้าไม่อยาก ครูจะไม่ใช้นะ | Sometimes I take photos or videos while we act or cook, and share them on {channels}. Would you like me to use photos of you? If not, I won’t. |
| If no / unsure | บันทึกว่า "ไม่ยินยอม" ขอบคุณน้อง และไม่ต้องถามซ้ำ | Record “Refused”, thank them, and don’t ask again. |
| Close | ขอบคุณที่บอกครูนะ | Thank you for telling me! |

If the child says no to health, staff record `refused` (the health row is deleted by the database; ⚖️ G9b lawyer question kept open, R-07) and talk with the
family before the next cooking class (D11).

---

## 5. Admin copy ✅ (rev) — split into area modules (R-17)

The block below is the **full reference** of every admin key in Thai and English. It is **not** built as one file.
Each card copies only its keys into its own module, so no card handles more than a few hundred lines and TH/EN stay
side by side:

| Module (create in card) | Top-level keys copied from the block below (type **and** `th` **and** `en`) | Exports |
|---|---|---|
| `src/content/admin/core.tsx` (P0-18) | `meta`, `common`, `login`, `shell`, `dashboard`, `labels`, `errors` (+ `errors.family_restricted` from § 5.1) | `type Labels`, `type AdminCore`, `adminCore` |
| `src/content/admin/inbox.tsx` (P1-12) | `inbox`, `request` (+ § 5.1 `request.lineHowTo`, `request.lineHint`) | `type AdminInbox`, `adminInbox` |
| `src/content/admin/settings.tsx` (P1-15) | `settings` | `type AdminSettings`, `adminSettings` |
| `src/content/admin/sessions.tsx` (P2-03) | `sessions`, `roster` (+ § 5.1 `sessions.cancelWarning`) | `type AdminSessions`, `adminSessions` |
| `src/content/admin/families.tsx` (P3-08) | `families`, `family` (+ § 5.1 `family.*` banners and `family.packages.close/void/expiry`) | `type AdminFamilies`, `adminFamilies` |
| `src/content/admin/payments.tsx` (P3-08) | `payments` (+ § 5.1 `payments.deleteSlipConfirm`) | `type AdminPayments`, `adminPayments` |
| `src/content/admin/privacy.tsx` (P5-02) | `privacy` (+ § 5.1 `privacy.*` owner-card keys) | `type AdminPrivacy`, `adminPrivacy` |
| `src/content/admin/health.tsx` (P4-15) | `health` (+ § 5.1 `health.*` roster states) | `type AdminHealth`, `adminHealth` |
| `src/content/admin/consents.tsx` (P4-15) | `consents`, `childScript` | `type AdminConsents`, `adminConsents` |
| `src/content/admin/media.tsx` (P4-15) | `media` | `type AdminMedia`, `adminMedia` |
| `src/content/admin/reports.tsx` (P6-03) | `reports` | `type AdminReports`, `adminReports` |

Pattern (each area module, e.g. `inbox.tsx`):

```tsx
import type { Lang } from "../site";
import type { Labels } from "./core"; // only if the area uses Labels<…>

export type AdminInbox = {
  inbox: { /* copy the `inbox` member of AdminContent below */ };
  request: { /* copy the `request` member */ };
};
const th: AdminInbox = { inbox: { /* th.inbox */ }, request: { /* th.request */ } };
const en: AdminInbox = { inbox: { /* en.inbox */ }, request: { /* en.request */ } };
export const adminInbox: Record<Lang, AdminInbox> = { th, en };
```

`src/content/admin/index.ts` (created in P0-18 with core only; **each area card adds one import, one `&` in the type
and one spread per language**, so these edits are serialized by `depends_on`):

```ts
import type { Lang } from "../site";
import { adminCore, type AdminCore } from "./core";
// import { adminInbox, type AdminInbox } from "./inbox";   ← added by P1-12, and so on

export type AdminContent = AdminCore; // & AdminInbox & AdminSettings & …
export const adminContent: Record<Lang, AdminContent> = {
  th: { ...adminCore.th }, // , ...adminInbox.th
  en: { ...adminCore.en }, // , ...adminInbox.en
};
```

`AdminLanguageProvider` keeps `import { adminContent, type AdminContent } from "@/content/admin";` (the folder's
`index.ts`), so the compiled admin code using `t.inbox.…`, `t.common.…` works unchanged. In `core.tsx`, the
`labels.consentPurpose` type imports `ConsentPurpose` from `@/lib/backend/common.schema` (exists from P0a); write
`labels.allergens` as `Labels<"peanut" | "tree_nut" | "milk" | "egg" | "wheat" | "flour" | "soy" | "fish" | "shellfish" |
"sesame">` instead of importing `Allergen` (the health schema only arrives in P4; round two adds `flour`). Status labels match
`00-overview.md` exactly (`new=ใหม่/New … spam=สแปม/Spam`). Function-valued keys take numbers or already-formatted dates.

```tsx
import type { Lang } from "./site";
import type { AdminErrorCode } from "@/lib/backend/result";
import type { Allergen } from "@/lib/backend/health.schema";
import type { ConsentPurpose } from "@/lib/backend/common.schema";

/**
 * Admin (staff) copy, Thai first (D23). REFERENCE ONLY (R-17): never create this as one file. Each card copies
 * its members (type + th + en) into src/content/admin/<area>.tsx per the module table above; core.tsx writes
 * labels.allergens as Labels<"peanut" | … | "sesame"> instead of importing Allergen.
 * Staff-facing, so plainer than the public site, but still warm and never blaming.
 */

type Labels<K extends string> = Record<K, string>;

export type AdminContent = {
  meta: { title: string };
  common: {
    save: string;
    saving: string;
    saved: string;
    cancel: string;
    close: string;
    edit: string;
    add: string;
    delete: string;
    confirm: string;
    back: string;
    loading: string;
    retry: string;
    copy: string;
    copied: string;
    search: string;
    all: string;
    yes: string;
    no: string;
    optional: string;
    none: string;
    prev: string;
    next: string;
    page: (n: number, of: number) => string;
    count: (n: number) => string;
    loadError: string;
    noHealthHint: string;
  };
  login: {
    title: string;
    lead: string;
    email: string;
    password: string;
    submit: string;
    submitting: string;
    failed: string;
    help: string;
  };
  shell: {
    skipLink: string;
    nav: {
      dashboard: string;
      inbox: string;
      sessions: string;
      families: string;
      payments: string;
      more: string;
      media: string;
      reports: string;
      privacy: string;
      settings: string;
    };
    roles: Labels<"owner" | "staff">;
    signedInAs: (email: string) => string;
    signOut: string;
    language: string;
    noAccess: { title: string; body: string };
    sessionEnded: string;
  };
  dashboard: {
    title: string;
    hello: (email: string) => string;
    newRequests: (n: number) => string;
    failedAlerts: (n: number) => string;
    nextSession: string;
    seatsLeft: (n: number) => string;
    noCapacity: string;
    pendingPayments: (n: number) => string;
    takedowns: (n: number) => string;
    dsrDue: (n: number) => string;
    allClear: string;
  };
  labels: {
    requestStatus: Labels<"new" | "contacted" | "waitlisted" | "converted" | "closed" | "spam">;
    notifyStatus: Labels<"pending" | "sent" | "failed" | "skipped">;
    sessionStatus: Labels<"draft" | "open" | "closed" | "cancelled">;
    familyStatus: Labels<"active" | "inactive">;
    packageCode: Labels<"single" | "four" | "eight" | "none">;
    preferredDay: Labels<"sat" | "sun" | "either" | "none">;
    contactPreference: Labels<"line" | "phone">;
    paymentMethod: Labels<"promptpay" | "bank_transfer" | "cash" | "other">;
    paymentStatus: Labels<"pending" | "paid" | "refunded" | "void">;
    purchaseStatus: Labels<"active" | "closed" | "void">;
    bookingStatus: Labels<"held" | "confirmed" | "attended" | "absent" | "cancelled" | "cancelled_late">;
    consentPurpose: Labels<ConsentPurpose>;
    consentDecision: Labels<"granted" | "refused" | "withdrawn">;
    consentGivenBy: Labels<"guardian" | "child">;
    consentChannel: Labels<"web" | "line" | "paper" | "in_person">;
    consentMethod: Labels<"checkbox" | "signature" | "staff_recorded_verbal">;
    consentState: Labels<"granted" | "needs_child" | "not_granted">;
    mediaLevel: Labels<"face_visible" | "face_hidden">;
    identityCheck: Labels<"in_person" | "line_account" | "phone_callback">;
    dsrRight: Labels<
      | "access"
      | "portability"
      | "objection"
      | "erasure"
      | "restriction"
      | "rectification"
      | "withdraw_consent"
    >;
    dsrChannel: Labels<"in_person" | "registered_mail" | "line" | "email" | "web">;
    dsrStatus: Labels<
      "received" | "in_review" | "waiting_for_requester" | "extended" | "completed" | "refused"
    >;
    breachRisk: Labels<"unlikely" | "risk" | "high">;
    mediaKind: Labels<"youtube" | "photo" | "other">;
    mediaStatus: Labels<"active" | "taken_down">;
    linkPurpose: Labels<"enrol" | "manage">;
    linkState: Labels<"active" | "used" | "expired" | "revoked">;
    allergens: Labels<Allergen>;
    lang: Labels<"th" | "en">;
  };
  inbox: {
    title: string;
    filters: Labels<
      "active" | "new" | "contacted" | "waitlisted" | "converted" | "closed" | "spam" | "all"
    >;
    searchPlaceholder: string;
    columns: Labels<
      "ref" | "received" | "name" | "children" | "package" | "when" | "status" | "alert"
    >;
    childrenCount: (n: number) => string;
    empty: { active: string; other: string; search: string };
    newBadge: (n: number) => string;
    alertFailed: string;
  };
  request: {
    title: (ref: string) => string;
    received: (when: string) => string;
    sections: Labels<"contact" | "children" | "interest" | "message" | "staff" | "alerts">;
    fields: Labels<
      | "name"
      | "phone"
      | "lineId"
      | "preferred"
      | "package"
      | "day"
      | "date"
      | "session"
      | "lang"
      | "notice"
      | "status"
      | "staffNote"
      | "handledBy"
      | "contactedAt"
    >;
    child: (n: number, nickname: string | null, age: number) => string;
    noNickname: string;
    noMessage: string;
    call: string;
    openLine: string;
    staffNoteHint: string;
    markContacted: string;
    markWaitlisted: string;
    markClosed: string;
    markSpam: string;
    reopen: string;
    spamConfirm: { title: string; body: string };
    convert: string;
    convertDialog: {
      title: string;
      lead: string;
      familyName: string;
      guardianName: string;
      /** R-38: phone and LINE ID are prefilled from the request and editable */
      guardianPhone: string;
      guardianLineId: string;
      /** R-38: shown when the request has neither (D26 LINE path); the guardian needs one of them */
      contactNeeded: string;
      childNickname: (n: number) => string;
      submit: string;
    };
    convertedTo: string;
    alerts: {
      none: string;
      attempt: (n: number) => string;
      resend: string;
      resent: string;
      failedHelp: string;
    };
  };
  sessions: {
    title: string;
    monthLabel: (isoMonth: string) => string;
    prevMonth: string;
    nextMonth: string;
    columns: Labels<
      "date" | "status" | "capacity" | "heldOffline" | "booked" | "seatsLeft" | "note"
    >;
    noCapacity: string;
    empty: string;
    generate: {
      open: string;
      title: string;
      lead: string;
      from: string;
      weeks: string;
      capacity: string;
      capacityHint: string;
      submit: string;
      done: (n: number) => string;
    };
    edit: {
      title: (date: string) => string;
      date: string;
      status: string;
      capacity: string;
      heldOffline: string;
      heldOfflineHint: string;
      publicNoteTh: string;
      publicNoteEn: string;
      publicNoteHint: string;
      staffNote: string;
      notWeekend: string;
    };
    openRoster: string;
  };
  roster: {
    title: (date: string) => string;
    summary: (booked: number, seatsLeft: number | null) => string;
    columns: Labels<
      "child" | "family" | "status" | "allergy" | "photos" | "in" | "out" | "actions"
    >;
    empty: string;
    checkIn: string;
    checkOut: string;
    markAbsent: string;
    cancel: string;
    cancelConfirm: { title: string; body: string };
    absentConfirm: { title: string; body: string };
    /** round two: notice fields (cancel and make-up). Shape of the TH and EN copy blocks below. */
    notice: {
      legend: string;
      line: string;
      inPerson: string;
      timeLabel: string;
      lineHint: string;
      inPersonHint: string;
      future: string;
      inTime: string;
      late: string;
      lead: (n: number) => string;
      cancelDone: { cancelled: string; cancelled_late: string };
    };
    pickupDialog: {
      title: (nickname: string) => string;
      lead: string;
      guardian: string;
      contact: string;
      none: string;
      submit: string;
    };
    addBooking: {
      open: string;
      title: string;
      family: string;
      child: string;
      package: string;
      noPackage: string;
      left: (n: number) => string;
      status: string;
      submit: string;
    };
    allergyNone: string;
    allergyUnknown: string;
    photoBadge: Labels<"granted" | "needs_child" | "not_granted">;
  };
  health: {
    view: string;
    logged: string;
    title: (nickname: string) => string;
    allergens: string;
    other: string;
    avoid: string;
    care: string;
    empty: string;
    noConsent: string;
    edit: string;

  };
  families: {

    searchPlaceholder: string;

    addTitle: string;
    columns: Labels<"family" | "children" | "guardian" | "credits" | "lastActivity">;
    empty: string;
    emptySearch: string;
  };
  family: {
    tabs: Labels<"overview" | "packages" | "bookings" | "consents" | "links">;

    contacts: string;

      | "identityMethod"

    contactForm: Labels<"name" | "phone" | "relationship" | "emergency" | "pickup" | "informed">;
    age: (years: number) => string;
    ageUnknown: string;
    /** age copied from the booking request until the birth month is known (children.reported_age_years) */
    reportedAge: (years: number) => string;

  payments: {

  media: {

        | "right"

      eraseBody: string;

    breach: {

        | "title"

        | "awareAt"
        | "riskLevel"

    };
    audit: {
      columns: Labels<"at" | "actor" | "action" | "table" | "row">;
      system: string;
      filterTable: string;
      empty: string;
    };
    /** storage_cleanup_queue: files SQL can't delete (listStorageCleanup / runStorageCleanup) */
    files: {
      lead: string;
      columns: Labels<"bucket" | "reason" | "queued" | "state">;
      reasons: Labels<"row_deleted" | "replaced">;
      pending: string;
      deleted: string;
      failed: string;
      run: string;

    };

  settings: {
    title: string;
    staff: {
      title: string;
      lead: string;
      columns: Labels<"email" | "role" | "lastSignIn">;
      noRole: string;

      addHint: string;
      add: string;
      remove: string;
      removeConfirm: { title: string; body: string };

    studio: {
      title: string;
      bookingFormOpen: string;

      staffAlertsEnabled: string;

      defaultCapacity: string;

    };
    texts: {
      title: string;
      lead: string;
      columns: Labels<"purpose" | "version" | "lang" | "effective" | "retired" | "checksum">;
      publish: string;
      publishTitle: string;
      publishLead: string;

        "purpose" | "titleTh" | "bodyTh" | "titleEn" | "bodyEn" | "isExplicit" | "childAge"
      >;
      retire: string;
      retireConfirm: { title: string; body: string };
    };
  };
  errors: Record<AdminErrorCode, string>;
};
```

### 5.1 Copy added by the October 2026 review (add to the named module, TH and EN together)

Studio voice: warm, plain, no blame, Thai ends with ค่ะ where natural. Lines marked OWNER-APPROVE describe what the
studio will do and need the owner's OK (G0 Q17).

| Module · key | TH | EN |
|---|---|---|
| core · `errors.family_restricted` | ครอบครัวนี้ระงับการใช้ข้อมูลอยู่ จึงทำรายการนี้ไม่ได้ค่ะ ติดต่อเจ้าของสตูดิโอ | This family's data is restricted, so this can't be done. Please ask the studio owner. |
| core · `dashboard.consentAlerts(n)` | มีการแจ้งเตือนเรื่องความยินยอมที่ยังไม่ได้ส่ง {n} รายการ | {n} consent alerts not sent yet |
| core · `dashboard.filesToDelete(n)` | ไฟล์รอลบ {n} ไฟล์ | {n} files waiting to be deleted |
| inbox · `request.lineHowTo(ref)` | รอผู้ปกครองส่งรหัส {ref} มาที่ LINE @cherryleeandco แล้วตอบในแชตนั้นค่ะ (บัญชีสตูดิโอทักหาใครก่อนไม่ได้) | Wait for the parent to send {ref} to LINE @cherryleeandco, then reply in that chat (the studio account can't message first). |
| inbox · `request.lineHint` | LINE ID ที่ผู้ปกครองให้ไว้ (ไว้อ้างอิงเท่านั้น ไม่ใช่สำหรับเพิ่มเพื่อน) | LINE ID the parent gave (for reference only, not for adding) |
| sessions · `sessions.cancelWarning(n)` | รอบนี้มีเด็กจองไว้ {n} คน ถ้ายกเลิกรอบ การจองทั้งหมดจะถูกยกเลิกและคืนสิทธิ์ให้ค่ะ อย่าลืมแจ้งครอบครัวทาง LINE นะคะ | {n} children are booked. Cancelling the session cancels their bookings and returns the credits. Remember to tell the families on LINE. |
| families · `family.restrictedBanner(date, note)` | ระงับการใช้ข้อมูลตั้งแต่ {date}: {note} ระหว่างนี้จองเรียน สร้างลิงก์ หรือแก้ข้อมูลไม่ได้ค่ะ | Data restricted since {date}: {note}. Bookings, links and profile edits are paused. |
| families · `family.restrictedShort` | ระงับการใช้ข้อมูลอยู่ | Data restricted |
| families · `family.scheduledDeletion(date)` | ไม่มีความเคลื่อนไหว 12 เดือน ระบบจะลบข้อมูลครอบครัวนี้วันที่ {date} | No activity for 12 months. This family's data will be deleted on {date}. |
| families · `family.linkChangesBanner(fields)` | ผู้ปกครองแก้ข้อมูลติดต่อผ่านลิงก์: {fields} กรุณาเช็กในแชต LINE ของครอบครัวก่อนยืนยันค่ะ | The parent changed contact details through the link: {fields}. Please check in the family's LINE chat before confirming. |
| families · `family.linkChangeOld(field, value)` | เดิม {field}: {value} | Before, {field}: {value} |
| families · `family.confirmLinkChanges` | เช็กแล้ว ยืนยันข้อมูลใหม่ | Checked, confirm the new details |
| families · `family.allergyRemoved(date)` | ข้อมูลการแพ้อาหารถูกลบเมื่อ {date} ถามครอบครัวก่อนทำอาหารนะคะ | Allergy info was removed on {date}. Check with the family before cooking. |
| payments · `payments.deleteSlipConfirm` | ลบรูปสลิปนี้? ข้อมูลการชำระเงินยังอยู่ ไฟล์จะถูกลบตอนกด "ลบไฟล์ที่รออยู่" | Delete this slip image? The payment record stays; the file is removed when you run "Delete queued files". |
| families · `family.packages.close` / `family.packages.void` | ปิดแพ็กเกจ / ยกเลิกแพ็กเกจ (เจ้าของ) | Close package / Void package (owner) |
| privacy · `privacy.ownerCardTitle` | ความเป็นส่วนตัว (เจ้าของ) | Privacy (owner) |
| privacy · `privacy.restrict` / `privacy.unrestrict` | ระงับการใช้ข้อมูล / ยกเลิกการระงับ | Restrict data use / Lift restriction |
| privacy · `privacy.restrictNoteLabel` | เหตุผลสั้นๆ (ห้ามใส่ข้อมูลสุขภาพ) | Short reason (no health details) |
| privacy · `privacy.restrictConfirm` | ระงับการใช้ข้อมูลครอบครัวนี้? ระหว่างนี้จะจอง สร้างลิงก์ หรือแก้ข้อมูลไม่ได้ ผู้ปกครองยังถอนความยินยอมได้ และระบบจะไม่ลบอัตโนมัติ | Restrict this family's data? Bookings, links and edits pause; the parent can still withdraw consent; nothing is auto-deleted. |
| privacy · `privacy.erasedThenFiles` | ลบข้อมูลครอบครัวแล้ว อย่าลืมกด "ลบไฟล์ที่รออยู่" ในหน้าความเป็นส่วนตัววันนี้นะคะ | Family data erased. Remember to run "Delete queued files" on the Privacy page today. |
| health · `health.noInfo` | ยังไม่มีข้อมูลการแพ้อาหาร ถามครอบครัวก่อนทำอาหารนะคะ | No allergy info — check with the family before cooking |
| health · `health.reportedNone(date)` | ผู้ปกครองแจ้งว่าไม่แพ้อะไร ({date}) | Parent reported no allergies ({date}) |
| health · `health.removedOn(date)` | ข้อมูลเดิมถูกลบเมื่อ {date} | Earlier info removed on {date} |
| health · `health.paperForm` | ดูแบบฟอร์มการแพ้อาหารที่สตูดิโอ | See the paper allergy form at the studio |
| core · `labels.notificationKind` (R-30, card P4-19) | `booking_request` คำขอจอง · `enrolment_submitted` ผู้ปกครองส่งแบบฟอร์มแล้ว · `consent_withdrawn` มีการถอนหรือไม่ให้ความยินยอม | Booking request · Enrolment form sent · Consent withdrawn or refused |
| core · `dashboard.alertsTitle` / `dashboard.openFamily` / `dashboard.resend` (card P4-19) | แจ้งเตือนที่ยังส่งไม่สำเร็จ / เปิดหน้าครอบครัว / ส่งอีกครั้ง | Alerts not sent yet / Open family / Resend |
| settings · `settings.systemCheck.title` / `.lead` (card P1-19) | ตรวจระบบ / เช็กว่าใส่รหัสลับครบหรือยัง ระบบไม่แสดงค่าของรหัสลับ | System check / Checks that every secret is set. Values are never shown. |
| settings · `settings.systemCheck.run` / `.present` / `.missing` / `.clientIp` | ตรวจตอนนี้ / ตั้งค่าแล้ว / ยังไม่ได้ตั้ง / ระบบเห็นที่อยู่ IP ของผู้เข้าชม | Check now / Set / Not set / Visitor IP is visible to the server |
| (public) `my-consents.tsx` · `inactiveChild` | ไม่ได้มาเรียนแล้ว ถอนความยินยอมได้ตามปกติค่ะ | No longer attending — you can still withdraw consent |
| (public) `my-consents.tsx` · `agreeToText` | ยินยอมตามข้อความด้านบน | I agree to the text above |

### 5.2 Copy and labels added on 14 October 2026

Every row is TH and EN, in the module named. Keys follow the existing modules in § 5. **Allergen labels are to confirm with the owner.** They are the ten keys of `ALLERGENS`: the nine major food allergens the owner asked for, plus `flour` (round two, decision 7, 14 Oct 2026). No Thai official list has been found for them. Lawyer review (G9a, G9b) covers the
privacy rows and the marketing text; the marketing text itself is the `consent_texts` row (`0006a`), not this table.

| Key | Module | TH | EN |
|---|---|---|---|

## 6. Accessibility, mobile and tests checklist

Every screen, before reporting done:
- [ ] Every input has a visible label; hints and errors are linked with `aria-describedby` (shadcn `FormControl`
      does this when `FieldError` is used).
- [ ] Errors: summary `role="alert"` on submit; field messages directly under the field; focus moves to the first error.
- [ ] Touch targets ≥ 44 × 44 px (`min-h-11`), including checkbox/radio rows, table links, tab triggers, menu items.
- [ ] Visible focus ring (site default `outline: 3px solid var(--dusty-ink)`); don't remove outlines.
- [ ] Text contrast ≥ 4.5:1: body `text-ink`/`text-ink-soft`, errors `text-cherry-deep`, never decorative colours.
- [ ] Status is text (or icon + text), never colour alone.
- [ ] `role="list"` on styled lists (`list-none`); headings in order (`h1` once per page).
- [ ] Thai: `<Nobr>` for compound words in headings/buttons; `lang="en"` on English inside Thai (`<En>`).
- [ ] Works at 360 px wide with no horizontal scroll; admin tables hide secondary columns or become cards.
- [ ] `prefers-reduced-motion` respected (no new animations needed; the global rule already shortens transitions).
- [ ] No consent box pre-ticked; one purpose per control; media boxes default off.
- [ ] Every consent control has its purpose `title` and **full** `body_md` visible in the DOM directly above it (no
      collapsible, no "read more", R-04); a test asserts `toBeVisible()` for each.
- [ ] No personal data in URLs, LINE deep links, toasts, `console.*` or analytics events.
- [ ] Admin pages `ssr: false` + noindex (inherited); `/enrol` and `/my` have `noindex` + `no-referrer`.
- [ ] `public/robots.txt` disallows `/admin`, `/enrol/`, `/my/`, `/pay/` (F0-03).

Tests to write (vitest + Testing Library, next to the component):
- `BookingForm` and `BookingThanks`: the 📝 test blocks in § 3.2 (honeypot `tabIndex=-1` in an `aria-hidden` wrapper;
  age select has no default; submit disabled without a Turnstile token (fake `window.turnstile`); every radio group has
  an accessible name; the notice links to `/privacy`; `preferred_missing` after choosing an age; thank-you focus on the
  heading and a LINE link with the ref code only).
- `HealthSection` ✅ (rev, above); `MediaSection`: every box unchecked on first render, every published text visible,
  unpublished purposes absent, the face-level radio has no default; `/my` row: full text visible, withdraw is one press.
- Roster health cell: the three states from `healthRosterState` render the right copy; `no_info` is the default when
  there is no record (R-07).
- `SubpageHeader`: language buttons expose `aria-pressed`; logo link goes to `/` or `/?lang=en`.
- Admin: `toAdminErrorCode` maps `"Unauthorized: …"` → `unauthorized` and unknown messages → `server_error`.

---

**Added 14 October 2026 (decisions 2, 3, 5, 8, 10):**

- `MarketingConsentBlock`: the full text is in the DOM above the checkbox (`toBeVisible`). The checkbox starts unchecked, also when the guardian already said yes. Withdraw is a button of at least 44 px, not a colour.
- Follow-up table: real `table` with column headers; the cards on phones keep their labels. No checkbox and no bulk control is in the DOM (test).
- Print page: `@media print` hides the shell and every button. Black on white. Signature lines are at least 12 mm tall. The print button announces its result in `aria-live="polite"`. The health block has a heading and is absent unless `includeHealth` (test).
- Allergen labels: the ten keys in `ALLERGENS` each have a TH and an EN label (test: no missing key, `flour` included). `ALLERGEN_SCREEN_GROUPS` contains every key exactly once (test), and the nut keys are one group. The comment in the code says the labels are to be confirmed with the owner.
- Make-up dialog: the notice value comes from settings and is read aloud in the lead text (no number in the code). Step a (notice) runs only for `held` or `confirmed` rows; `cancelled_late` stops the dialog.
- Cancel dialog (round two): the notice time cannot be in the future (test). The channel is `line` or `in_person` only: no web form (test). The toast uses `notice.cancelDone[status]`.
- Print route (round two): the allergen checklist is in the DOM for every print, with or without the tick, and it has ten rows. Clearing the health tick removes only the foods-to-avoid and care-notes lines from the DOM (test); the checklist stays. The print sheet has no interactive checkbox.
- Follow-up list (round two): one row per granted guardian (two rows for two guardians); the label is "ครอบครัวน้อง" + nickname and "Family of" + nickname; the page has no full name, age, health data or LINE ID (test).
